# Consuela Dashboard — Agent Operational Manual

> **For the AI Coding Agent (Kilo) only.**  
> This is your single source of truth when a user asks how to **use**, **configure**, **troubleshoot**, or **extend** the live Consuela family dashboard.  
> Always start here before answering operational questions. Cross-reference the linked deep docs.  
> **Mandatory:** After any code change that touches UI, navigation, meals, emergency, or integrations, update this file in the same session.

**Current Dashboard Snapshot** (maintain on every relevant change)  
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-27 | **All demo/seed data purged — dashboard starts completely clean.** Every code-level seeder and PB fallback for demo data was removed (tasks, meals, grocery, pantry, recipes, rewards, penalties, schedules, emergency contacts, suggestions, briefings). The live PocketBase was purged of 117 demo records (42 tasks, 5 penalties, 32 grocery, 5 schedules, 2 emergency contacts, 11 pantry, 3 suggestions, 15 briefings, 1 snapshot, 1 skill-tree profile). Preserved: 9 real family members, 67 Google Calendar events, 3 service-config keys. A one-time localStorage data epoch reset (`consuela-data-epoch`, epoch 1) in `SyncInit.tsx` wipes stale per-device caches on first load after deploy. Every screen starts empty with the existing empty-state UI; the user adds all real data themselves. Emergency fallback contacts in `fallback-data.ts` kept as-is (placeholder info — user re-adds in Settings). `membersFallback` kept as-is (real family).
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-27 | **Birds fly around the sun on clear days — passing behind it as they go.** A small (Not Boring)-style detail: three tiny seagull silhouettes (two-arc SVG glyphs, 8–10px wide, `skin.ink` at 55% opacity) circle the sun on a flattened elliptical flight path — crossing left→right in front of the sun, then swinging around behind it where they fade out (opacity 0 on the far half of `wxBirdOrbit`, scaled down to 0.8 for distance) before re-emerging at the left side. Each bird flaps like a distant seagull: an SMIL `<animate attributeName="d">` morphs the wing path between the wings-up and wings-down shapes (0.9–1.1s per flap, spline-eased — SMIL instead of CSS `d` so it works in every browser). Flight periods 17/23/20s with negative delays staggering the flock. The layer lives inside the sun element, so the flock automatically tracks the sun arc and timeline scrubbing. Data-true gating: birds appear only when the sky is genuinely clear — day + `cloud_cover < 30` + no rain/snow/storm codes + no fog — and fade in/out over 1.2s as conditions change. Reduced motion renders static silhouettes at fixed positions with no flap (the animate element is not rendered). Test hook: `data-testid="wx-birds"`. Verified: typecheck clean, eslint clean, suite 634/634 (+3 bird tests), feature probe 16/16 (birds visible on clear sky, hidden under 90% cloud cover, orbit + flap verified in a real browser).
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-27 | **Weather scene completes the Not Boring trifecta — realtime visibility fog, procedural clouds, true moon phases.** The three (Not Boring) Weather signature features the scene was missing are now in. (1) **Realtime simulation completed:** the scene now fetches and renders real `visibility` (Open-Meteo current + hourly, free field) — fog banks derive from actual visibility meters (<8km scales fog opacity up to 0.55; <1km = dense), replacing the humidity≥82% proxy (kept only as a weak fallback when visibility data is absent; WMO fog codes 45/48 still trigger). (2) **Procedural clouds:** the 4 fixed SVG cloud shapes are gone — each cloud is now procedurally generated as a cluster of 4–9 overlapping ellipses from a seeded PRNG (mulberry32) keyed to the hour timestamp, so every hour's clouds are unique and stable within the hour; blob count/size grow with `cloud_cover`; scrubbing the timeline regenerates the sky's clouds for that hour with a 1.1s crossfade (`wxFadeIn`, previously unused). And yes — **tap the clouds to build your own**: tapping a visible cloud puffs a new blob into it at the tap point (`wxPuff` 0.9s grow-and-fade, max 3 concurrent puffs, reduced-motion disables the toy). Clouds are tappable because the card's content overlay is now `pointer-events-none` with `pointer-events-auto` only on the unit toggle, day strip, and Details button (modal hero overlay likewise). (3) **True moon phases:** the fixed crescent is replaced by a real synodic model (29.53058867-day month from the Jan 6 2000 18:14 UTC new-moon epoch) — the night moon renders tonight's actual illuminated fraction via a terminator path (waxing lit right, waning lit left, earthshine dim disc beneath), glow scales with illumination, and the modal's Daylight card gained a dotted-leader **Moon row** ("Full Moon · 100%"). New exports from `WeatherScene.tsx` for testing: `moonPhase`, `moonPhaseName`, `moonPathD`, `mulberry32`, `makeCloudSpec`. New Open-Meteo field: `visibility` (current + hourly). Verified: typecheck clean, eslint clean, suite 631/631 (+11 tests: moon model ×4, procedural clouds ×4, visibility fog ×2, night-scene moon path ×1), redesign probe ALL CHECKS PASSED, new deterministic feature probe `scripts/consuela/verify-weather-features.mjs` 11/11 (mocked Open-Meteo: visibility in fetch URL, 4 unique multi-blob clouds at 90% cover, tap-to-puff adds an animated blob, fog at 800m / none at 16km, moon path at forced night, modal Moon row, 0 page errors).

Modal hourly chips dropped boxy borders for open Not Boring-style columns (active hour = accent top bar + tint; precip always rendered but invisible below 20% instead of a stray "·"), modal hero numerals grew 54→60px, card/modal "°" marks enlarged. - **Last Updated:** 2026-08-27 | **Home weather card redesigned in "(Not Boring) Weather"'s visual language — data-true scene, timeline spine, pastel season skins by day, full Not Boring dark after real sunset.** The weather card is no longer a static seasonal illustration with a corner icon: it's now a living scene rendered from real Open-Meteo data — sun/moon riding the real sun arc (API sunrise/sunset), cloud count/opacity tracking `cloud_cover`, rain/snow particles driven by precipitation probability and slanted by wind speed/direction, fog from humidity, lightning flashes on storm codes. The huge SF Pro black-weight temperature (56–64px, tabular-nums, count-up on change) sits centered with condition, H/L, and the next-hours outlook beneath; the card's bottom fifth is the **day strip** — rest-of-day temp curve with rain ticks (≥40%), NOW + 3-hour labels, and press/drag/keyboard to preview any hour (scene, temp, and condition follow the thumb; release animates back to now). The Details modal is now a timeline machine: live scene + huge temp for the scrubbed hour (scrub past sunset and the sky darkens), a **24h scrubber** (`role="slider"` with spoken `aria-valuetext`, rain band, drag + arrow keys/Home/End), exploded **dotted-leader rows** (Humidity ··· 62%, Wind ··· 8 mph WSW, Precipitation, Cloud cover; Feels like / UV 5-dot scale / Pressure shown for the current hour only — missing data hides rows), a **sun arc** with sunrise/sunset + daylight length, and an **Hourly | Daily** toggle (24 scrollable hour chips ↔ the existing 5-day range-bar list). Palettes: four pastel day skins auto-keyed to season (spring pastel lemon/lilac, summer pastel guava/peach, autumn soft red/amber, winter ice blue/silver — near-black ink on pastel, no user picker) and full Not Boring night keyed to real `is_day`: near-black `#060609→#141420` sky, white numerals, one condition accent (storm amber / rain cyan / snow ice / fog gray / default soft red). Skies crossfade (5 stacked layers, 1.1s), temps count up (550ms cubic ease-out), scene layers morph while scrubbing — all CSS-only, all neutralized under `prefers-reduced-motion` (JS matchMedia gate + CSS kill block). Holiday overlays survive on top of the new scene (`SeasonHolidayArt backdrop={false}` + holiday particles). New free Open-Meteo fields wired: `is_day, cloud_cover, wind_direction_10m, uv_index, pressure_msl` (current) + hourly `is_day/cloud_cover/wind/humidity` + daily `sunrise/sunset/uv_index_max`. The °F pill is now a real unit-toggle button. New files: `WeatherSkins.ts`, `WeatherScene.tsx`, `WeatherParticles.tsx` (extracted verbatim); `WeatherWidget.tsx` rewritten; `DESIGN.md` created (dashboard world + weather-card world + direction contract in the spec). Verified: typecheck clean, eslint clean on all 5 touched files, suite 620/620 (+4 new weather tests: strip slider + rain ticks, keyboard preview + return-to-now, night skin, modal scrubber/UV), Playwright 30/30 — 1440 day (card = 350px cell, press-preview shows "7 PM · Clear" then returns to now, modal scrub/daily-toggle/ESC), forced night (near-black sky + white numerals), 390 phone (no overflow).
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-27 | **"Consuela suggests" widget no longer spills onto the widget below.** The suggestions card rendered up to 3 tall rows (title + body + action button) with no containment, so on the 350px bento cells the content overflowed the card and bled into the widget underneath. - **Last Updated:** 2026-08-27 | **Tasks page polished to Home-grade + 15 confirmed backend bugs fixed.** UI: pending and completed task rows now use the Home `schedule-row liquid-glass` recipe (glowing priority rail — rose/amber/mint, color-tinted frosting, gradient glass `+pts`/`Done` pills; swipe/tap/keyboard-complete behavior all preserved); member filter chips now use the shared `.member-chip` pill (the Calendar recipe, single CSS source of truth) with `Avatar size="xs" variant="emoji"` member-color circles; the Tasks ⇄ Leaderboard switch gained the Calendar active-tab treatment via a new opt-in `emphasize` prop on `SegmentedControl` (solid accent-gradient pill, white text, glow — Tasks page only; every other SegmentedControl in the app keeps the quiet surface slide). Backend fixes (all audit-confirmed): **streaks** now per-member and today's completions count (date-part compare in `getThisWeeksCompletedDates`, optional member filter); **week rollover** now archives the finished week AND records the Hall of Fame winner + previous-week ranks on both the live-interval path and the reload path (idempotent mount backfill — weeks that rolled over while the page was closed get enshrined once); **recurring regen** no longer compounds duplicates (lineage dedupe by title+cadence+assignee, stale prior-week completed sources removed, one pending clone per lineage); **reward redemption** balance-checked at entry and submit with honest "needs N more pts" errors instead of silent no-op + fake success; **undo** pushes the reversal adjust tx to PB immediately and the claim guard now treats a reversed earn as released (undo actually frees the universal task for re-claiming); **claim errors** differentiated (409 race w/ winner name / 404 not-synced-yet / 400 not-universal / 401 PIN / generic) instead of everything reading "already grabbed", and successful claims adopt the server's weekData when it's fresher; **claim route** writes full completion fields (`status:"done"`, completedBy/At/InWeek, `assigned`) to the task row and validates universal/not-done before scoring; **snapshot restore** preserves real ids, assignee emojis, and completion attribution (was regenerating ids and wiping completedBy) and merges richer same-week weekData from other devices; **snapshot sync** failures now surface (`res.ok` check + honest 400/502 from the route instead of masked `{ok:true}`); **`syncTasksToPB`** now writes `assigned` + `status` + completion fields so the Home pending-tasks widget actually sees Tasks-page tasks (and stops rewriting `createdAt`); **Hall of Fame PB sync** idempotent (dedupes by member+weekStart); **`consuela_data_snapshots`** added to the canonical pb-seed. Dead code removed (unused Chip/ListRow/ErrorState/LEVELS imports, a dead ternary, hardcoded amber/emerald palette → accent tokens in Recent Activity + champion streak pill + needs-parent label). Verified: typecheck clean, eslint clean on touched files, suite 603/603 (+11 new task-utils tests: streak date/member filtering, regen no-compound/lineage-dedupe/tracker semantics), build clean.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **/meals member-strip hydration mismatch fixed + signed-in custom avatars now land.** Root cause: the module-load hydrate in `db/index.ts` warms `membersCache` from PocketBase **on the server** (admin PB access), so SSR rendered the real PB members (Jeffery's customized 😎) while the client's first render still had an empty cache (the browser roster fetch via `/api/members/admin` is async and 401s for guests) and rendered the static fallback (👨) — PlanTab's pre-mount branch read `db.selectMembers()`, which stopped being deterministic once that PB warm-up landed. Fix: new `db.selectMembersFallback()` (never reflects the warmed cache) now backs PlanTab's pre-mount render, so server HTML and client hydration are identical; PlanTab also listens for `consuela-members-updated` — now dispatched by the client hydrate on a **successful** roster fetch only (a fallback-only cache must not fire it: useAuth treats a missing member as deleted and signs out) — so the strip swaps from fallbacks to the real family when the roster lands (before this the `[mounted]` memo froze at whatever the cache held at mount, so signed-in users never saw custom avatars in the strip). Guests keep the honest fallback family. Verified: typecheck clean, suite 592/592 (+3 tests: node-env SSR determinism + no-PB-leak `renderToString`, jsdom roster swap), eslint 0 on touched files; Playwright guest + signed-in at 390px — 0 hydration errors on `/meals` + all three legacy deep links, signed-in strip shows Jeffery's real 😎 after mount.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-31 | **Multi-store grocery with live price comparison + Composio integration.** The 🛒 Shop step is now store-aware: every grocery item has a `store` field (default "aldi") and a colored pill showing which store it's assigned to. Tap the pill to open a modal store picker — six pinned stores (Aldi, Meijer, Walmart, Target, Family Fare, Costco) plus 11 others discovered via Composio's Instacart toolkit for Holland 49423. A "Compare Prices" button opens a `PriceCompareSheet` modal that fetches per-store prices for all items via Hermes, shows a grid of item × store prices with per-store totals, highlights the cheapest split, and reports total savings vs worst-case. "Apply to all" sets the cheapest store as default for every item. Walmart items get a separate local list + walmart.com search link since Walmart is NOT on Instacart in Holland. Composio is the primary shopping list backend (4 tools: `create_shopping_list`, `add_items_to_shopping_list`, `get_shopping_list_details`, `remove_items_from_shopping_list`) with a direct API fallback. Consuela can now suggest `grocery_store_optimization` (category isle clustering across pinned stores, pantry-low + buy-again detection). New files: `src/lib/stores.ts` (store registry + `calculateCheapestSplit`), `StorePill.tsx`, `StorePicker.tsx`, `PriceCompareSheet.tsx`; tests: `composio-instacart.test.ts` (2), `price-compare.test.ts` (6). Verified: typecheck clean, all 8 new tests pass.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **Grocery→Pantry handoff fixed for guests + expired sessions (local-first kitchen writes).** Root cause: the session-auth migration gated all `/api/db/*` writes behind the `consuela_session` cookie, but the kitchen hooks still gated UI state on PB write success — guests got 401 on every write, so "Send N to pantry" failed all items and checked rows stayed stuck on the list; compounding, live PB `grocery_list_items.userId` was required while most create paths never sent it (grocery creates 400'd even signed-in). Fix: `usePantry` (addPantryItem now local-first — PB write best-effort, the item always lands in pantry state + localStorage; update/remove state-first; normalizeName guarded against missing names; nameless PB rows filtered on load), `useGrocery` (add/toggle/update/delete/override all optimistic-local + best-effort PB; deleteGroceryItem falls back to delete-by-name when the local numeric id doesn't exist in PB, so fallback items materialized by a toggle no longer resurrect on reload), `db/index.ts` + `pb-db.ts` default `userId: "demo"` on grocery create, hermes-tools chat grocery create sends userId, `pb-seed.ts` declares userId optional. Live PB patched: `grocery_list_items.userId` no longer required. No visual change — the kitchen tabs stay calm input surfaces. Verified: typecheck clean, suite 569/569, 0 new lint; Playwright guest + signed-in — "🥫 Sent 5 items to pantry", checked rows clear, items appear in pantry, PB grocery ends 0 rows / pantry 5.
- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **Calendar page restyled to match the Home widget glass.** Every major Calendar surface (hero greeting, month grid, Today panel, Upcoming panel, Family Routines panel, Add/Edit form cards, and the upcoming day cards) now uses the shared `WidgetCard` pastel-bento shell — the exact same material as the Home widgets — with per-card tones from the Home palette (month grid cool blue `#3b82f6`, Today + Routines ice blue `#22d3ee`, Upcoming mint `#10b981`, forms violet `#8b5cf6`, hero follows the chosen accent). Event rows frost with their own event color, routine cards with their routine color, and active member filter chips now glow in that member's own color instead of the global accent. The hardcoded-cyan Sync button now follows the accent. Month-grid day cells are now visible unselected glass tiles (tone-tinted fill + hairline tone border + inset top highlight in dark and light) instead of near-invisible 3% white, with a crisper accent border on Today and a white edge on the selected day. The member filter chips' emojis now sit on the same `Avatar` pill as the Home family strip (28px member-color circle + tone ring) instead of overflowing a bare 21.6px dot. The month grid now borrows Apple Calendar's signatures: a larger lighter month title with ghost chevrons flanking a rounded "Today" pill (‹ Today ›), today's date number inside a filled accent circle with a matching glow (tile tint softened to support it), and slightly bigger event dots (4.2px). When today is the selected day both signals stay visible: the selected tile becomes a clear translucent accent box (20–22% fill + white inner ring, no dense gradient) and the today circle keeps its solid accent fill with a white ring — day cells now carry `is-selected` and `is-today` together instead of either/or. The Calendar ⇄ Schedule tab pills now use a solid accent-gradient fill with white text + accent glow for the active tab (was a 24% accent wash that blended into the glass track), so the highlighted tab is unmistakable in dark and light. Google-synced events are now month-accurate: they carry month/year (new `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts`), the sync no longer drops events outside the current month, and the grid/Today/Upcoming panels only show each event in its own month — no more ghost copies when navigating months. Console hygiene fixed: the anti-FOUC theme script moved from a raw `<script dangerouslySetInnerHTML>` to `next/script strategy="beforeInteractive"` (React 19 no longer warns; still injected into the server HTML head and runs pre-hydration — verified data-theme set at domcontentloaded), and the Calendar page's localStorage-seeded events/schedules no longer cause a hydration mismatch (render uses the deterministic fallbacks until mount via a `useSyncExternalStore` mounted flag, then swaps to stored data). Verified: Home `.widget-card` and Calendar grid card are byte-identical in computed style in both dark and light; typecheck clean, 0 new lint, suite 560/560, no page errors or horizontal overflow at 390/1440, no hydration/script-tag console errors even with stored events differing from fallbacks.

- **Last Updated:** 2026-08-26 | **Comprehensive backend audit — gateway unblocked, unauthenticated-PB reads fixed, dead/dangerous routes removed.** Follow-up sweep across all 78 API routes found and fixed a second class of breakage beyond the Integrations repair. **Root cause #1 (biggest):** NO PB collection had the standard `created`/`updated` autodate fields, but the sessioned gateway `/api/db/[collection]` sorts by `-created` by default — so EVERY browser-side gateway read 400'd at PB → bare empty-body 500 → the dual-mode client silently fell back to stale local cache (cross-device sync dead for all gateway collections). Fix: `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` now injects `created`/`updated` autodate fields into every collection (new `withAutodate()` + shared `buildField()` used by both create and patch paths); re-ran `npm run pb:seed` → all 37 collections patched. Gateway routes also hardened to return parseable JSON errors (`db_error` 502 / `invalid_json` 400) instead of empty 500s. **Root cause #2:** several server libs still used the unauthenticated `getPB()` client, which 403s on every locked collection. New `getAuthedPB()` helper in `src/lib/pb-auth.ts` (same cached admin client/token refresh as `withAdmin`); switched `skill-tree.ts` (was 500 "Failed to load skill tree data"), `schedule-analytics.ts` (was 500 "Only superusers…"), `recurring-patterns.ts`, and `family-memory.ts` to it — all now return real data. `family-memory` also needed its collection seeded (new `consuela_family_memories`, 37th collection). **Root cause #3:** formData routes (`ocr/extract`, `voice/process`, `photo/process`) threw Next's raw 500 on non-multipart requests and queried a phantom `users` collection via unauthenticated client — now content-type-guarded (clean 400), `users`→`members`, `consuela_saved_locations` read wrapped in fallback, all on `withAdmin`. `email/forward` got the same `users`→`members` + `withAdmin` fix (now 200 end-to-end). **Removed:** legacy `/api/family` route + `family.json` (zero callers; leaked plaintext PINs and allowed unauthenticated arbitrary file writes) and the dead `/api/consuela/chat` stub + its test (unauthenticated PB, phantom `conversations`/`conversation_messages`, hardcoded TODO reply — real chat uses `/api/hermes/chat`). **chat/process hardened:** `/api/chat/process` (enhanced-AI clarification layer, called by `UnifiedInput` before `/api/hermes/chat`) 500'd on phantom `consuela_family_members`/`consuela_saved_locations`; now those reads fall back to `[]` and the ambiguity check is skipped when no family context loads, so it passes through cleanly (200, no clarification spam) and chat still flows to Hermes. **Known limitations (left as graceful degradation, not crashes):** money-mountain + time-capsule collections were never seeded and their schemas use `relation` fields to a nonexistent `users` collection — both degrade to empty 200s (data doesn't persist); the enhanced-AI family (`family-ai`/`consuela-ai-enhanced` context enrichment) is a parallel demo built on phantom collections and only its crash paths were fixed. Verified live: all GET routes 200 (or deliberate 404 on removed routes), gateway reads 200, skill-tree/schedule-analytics/recurring-patterns/family-memory return real data, family-memory create/read/delete round-trips. Suite 555/555, `tsc` clean, eslint clean on all touched files.

- **IMPORTANT BUILD NOTE:** After every `npm run build`, the CSS chunks can go out of sync (Next.js 16.2.6 Turbopack bug). If the dashboard loads with broken layout (big icons, wrong nav styles), **restart the container**: `docker restart consuela-dashboard`. This is faster than a rebuild.
- **Navigation model:** Floating bottom-center glass capsule nav (Home, Ask, Meals, Tasks, Calendar, House, Settings) where the active item expands into a neon-lime pill, plus always-visible floating red Emergency shield button on Home
- **Deploy runbook (LOCAL ONLY — gitignored, contains NAS SSH credentials):**
`DEPLOY_NAS_LOCAL.md` in this repo root. Read it before any deploy/update of the live dashboard.
It covers: SSH access to the QNAP, tar-over-ssh sync (never rsync), docker build requirements
(--build-arg NEXT_PUBLIC_PB_URL), container swap, rollback twin, PB lockdown command, and 8
verified gotchas. **Tech surface for ops:** Next.js 16 + React 19 + Tailwind CSS 4, PocketBase-backed database (`pb-db.ts` + `db/index.ts`), API routes under `src/app/api/`, custom SVG animations (no framer-motion)
- **PocketBase migration:** **Complete (2026-06-18).** All 8 phases of the localStorage→PB migration have been implemented. PB is now the primary data source for members, events, schedules, grocery, pantry, meals, recipes, tasks, rewards, penalties, week data, family goals, hall of fame, emergency contacts, and auth sessions. localStorage is retained as a per-device cache/fallback. A global `CacheRefresher` component (in `layout.tsx`) polls PB every 60s and on visibility change to keep cross-device state in sync. The PB seed defines 18 collections total (6 original + 12 new).

---

## 1. User Interface (UI) Navigation

### 1.1 Current Layout & Navigation Model

The dashboard is a mobile-first, glass-morphism, bottom-nav app with a persistent floating action for emergencies.

**Capsule Nav (always present, `CapsuleNav.tsx`):** a floating bottom-center glass capsule. The active item expands into a neon-lime pill (green icon circle + label); inactive items are compact dark circles. Width expansion is a CSS-only `0fr→1fr` grid-column transition; the whole capsule auto-scales down on narrow viewports (`--capsule-scale`). Seven items for adults: Home, Ask, Meals, Tasks, Calendar, House (`/ha`, Home Assistant controls), Settings — the House item is hidden for the child role (kid mode keeps the original 6 tabs). Items are `<button>`s with `aria-label` + `aria-current="page"`.

| Label          | Route          | Icon (active = heavier stroke / filled)          | Primary Notes |
|----------------|----------------|--------------------------------------------------|---------------|
| Home           | `/`            | House                                            | Dashboard with weather, today's events, quick AI ask, weekly meals preview, tasks |
| Ask            | `/chat`        | Speech bubble with 3 dots (primary, filled when active) | Main conversational interface |
| Meals          | `/meals`       | Pot / plate                                      | Kitchen loop: 🍽️ Plan (weekly meal grid + inline Recipe box) → 🛒 Shop (store-aware grocery list + price comparison + Composio) → 🥫 Stock (pantry + low/out → grocery sync) |
| Tasks          | `/tasks`       | Checklist                                        | Chore list with points |
| Calendar       | `/calendar`    | Calendar grid (rect + binding rings + day dots) | Family routines, events, and month view |
| House          | `/ha`        | Sliders (3 vertical faders)               | Home Assistant controls: Overview, Security (arm/disarm), Climate, Lights, Automations. Hidden for kids (child role) |
| Settings       | `/settings`    | Gear                                             | Far-right tab. Theme (dark/light/system + 10 accents + high-contrast), family members, emergency contacts config |

**Floating Emergency Button (always on Home, `EmergencyButton.tsx`):**
- Fixed position: `top-4 right-4`, `z-50`
- Red rose-500 circle with white shield + exclamation SVG
- Taps → modal with 4 serious types: 🔥 Fire, 💧 Water Leak, 🤕 Injury, 🚨 General
- On selection → POST `/api/emergency` with PIN from auth context → dual notification (SMS via carrier email-to-SMS + HTML email via Gmail App Password)
- **PIN required:** The endpoint verifies the PIN against stored family member PINs. Unauthenticated requests return 401.
- Success state: green check + "Alert Sent" + auto-close after 3s + count of successful deliveries
- Failure: red X + "Try Again" + advice to call 911 directly

**Recent Visual Language (as of adbe770):**
- Glass / translucent surfaces with 20px blur
- Isometric cards with perspective hover lift (`isometric-card` class)
- Gradient orbs as decorative background elements (pulse + melt animations)
- Safe-area insets respected for iOS notch / home indicator

**How to document future UI updates (mandatory for the agent):**
Use this exact delta format in the "What's New" area and update 1.5 journeys:

```markdown
### UI Change Record — 2026-06-12 — Liquid Glass cards for Today / Daily Schedule / Tasks
- Added / Changed: `src/app/page.tsx` (Today + Tasks items), `src/components/ui/ScheduleDisplay.tsx` (Schedule items + empty state)
- Visual / Motion: New `.liquid-glass` class in `src/app/globals.css`. Each row is now a 3D "bubble": color-tinted linear-gradient frosting (rgba 0.32 → 0.16 across 135°), 20px backdrop blur, 1.25rem squircle radius, bright `inset 0 1px 0` top highlight + soft `inset 0 -1px 0` bottom edge, 0/8/24 drop shadow that deepens to 0/14/32 on hover with a 3px lift. Light-mode variant swaps the inner highlight to a brighter 0.85 white edge and softens the drop shadow. Accent bar shrunk from w-1 to w-0.5 and given an 8px currentColor glow. Member / points pills moved from solid `surface-3` to `glass-subtle`. Reduced-motion disables the lift.
- Color frosting sources: Today events use the event color (mint/violet/amber/cyan/rose/nori). Tasks use priority (rose > 15pts, amber > 10pts, mint default). Daily Schedule uses the schedule item color.
- Agent action required: Update this section + Common Journeys + any affected SOP
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "On the Home screen the Today events, Daily Schedule, and Tasks cards now feel like little 3D bubbles — each row has a frosted glass surface with a light frosting of its existing color, a bright top highlight, and a soft drop shadow that lifts the card off the background. Hover to see them gently rise."
- Added / Changed: <component or page>
- Visual / Motion: <describe exactly what user sees, file paths, CSS classes>
- Agent action required: Update this section + Common Journeys + any affected SOP
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "On the Home screen the chat bubble now gently floats up and down..."
```

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-27 — Weather scene hydration mismatch fixed (moon path)
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — the card scene's `timestamp` fallback was `new Date().getTime()` at render time; during SSR (weather data not yet fetched) the server computed the moon phase a few ms earlier than the client's first render, so `moonPathD` emitted a terminator radius differing in the 8th decimal (`15.99998662…` vs `15.99998664…`) → React hydration mismatch on `[data-testid="wx-moon-lit"]`. Fix: the pre-data fallback timestamp is now the deterministic constant `0` (`activeHour ? hourTime : weatherData ? Date.now() : 0`) — server HTML and client first render are byte-identical; the real timestamp (and true moon phase) lands with the client-side fetch a moment later. The cloud seed was already hour-quantized and unaffected; `sunProgressAt` already returns a fixed 0.5 pre-data; the modal scene is client-only and untouched.
- Visual / Motion: No visual change — the pre-data frame (a fraction of a second) shows the deterministic fallback scene; the true moon phase appears with the data, exactly as before, minus the console error.
- Color sources: None — logic-only fix.
- Agent action required: Update this section + Change Log. Convention: any scene value derived from wall-clock time must be deterministic (constant or quantized) until client-only data lands.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Fixed a console error on the weather card: the moon's shape was being computed slightly differently between the server and your browser (milliseconds apart), which React flagged as a mismatch. The card now renders identically on both and the real moon phase appears the instant the weather data loads. Nothing looks different — the error is just gone."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-27 — Weather scene: birds circle the sun on clear days — seagull flap + pass behind the sun
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherScene.tsx` (`BIRDS` config — 3 birds with elliptical flight amplitudes (`a` 44/58/50px horizontal, `b` 12/16/14px vertical), periods 17/23/20s, negative delays −3/−11/−16s, sizes 10/8/9px, flap rates 0.9/1.1/1.0s, static fallback positions — plus a `birdsOut` gate: day + `cloudCover < 30` + no rain/snow/storm codes + `fogOpacity === 0` + animated. The sun branch carries a `data-testid="wx-birds"` layer inside the sun element, so the flock tracks the sun arc and timeline scrubbing automatically. **Flight:** each bird flies a flattened ellipse via `wxBirdOrbit` — crossing left→right in front of the sun (0–50%, fully visible), then swinging up and around behind the disc where it fades to opacity 0 and scales down to 0.8 (50–90%, the "behind the sun" half), re-emerging at the left side (90–100%). **Flap:** each bird is a two-arc seagull silhouette (`skin.ink` stroke at 55% opacity) whose wing path morphs between wings-up and wings-down shapes via SMIL `<animate attributeName="d">` (spline-eased, works in every browser — no CSS `d` dependency); the container fades over 1.2s as conditions change; reduced motion renders static silhouettes at fixed positions with no animate element), `src/app/globals.css` (`wxBirdOrbit` keyframes — elliptical translate + behind-sun fade + depth scale, driven by per-bird `--wx-bird-a`/`--wx-bird-b` vars; added to the reduced-motion kill block; the earlier scaleY `wxBirdFlap` was removed in favor of the SMIL path morph), `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` (mock payload gained `cloud`/`code` overrides; +3 tests: birds visible on a clear day, hidden under 90% cloud cover, hidden when raining), `scripts/consuela/verify-weather-features.mjs` (+5 checks: birds hidden under 90% cover, visible on clear sky, three birds, all on the wxBirdOrbit flight path, three SMIL flap animates — probe now 16/16).
- Visual / Motion: On clear days three small seagulls circle the sun the way distant gulls do — flapping steadily (wings morphing up and down), crossing in front of the sun from left to right, then disappearing as they swing behind it and fading back in on the other side. They only appear when the sky is genuinely clear (low cloud cover, no precipitation, no fog) and fade away when clouds roll in. Flights are slow (17–23s per circuit), flaps quick (~0.9–1.1s); under `prefers-reduced-motion` the birds remain as static silhouettes.
- Color sources: bird strokes use `skin.ink` at 55% opacity — correct on every seasonal day skin automatically; night excluded by the gate.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "On clear days, three small seagulls now fly around the sun — flapping their wings like gulls seen from far away. They cross in front of the sun from left to right, then disappear as they swing behind it and reappear on the other side. When clouds roll in (or rain, fog, or sunset), they're gone — they only come out when the sky is genuinely clear."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-27 — Weather scene: realtime visibility fog, procedural tap-to-puff clouds, true moon phases
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherScene.tsx` (the three (Not Boring) signature features: **(1) Realtime visibility fog** — `SceneState` gained `visibility: number | null` + `timestamp: number`; fog opacity now derives from real visibility meters (≥8km = none; scales to 0.55 as visibility → 0), WMO fog codes 45/48 still trigger, and the old humidity≥82% heuristic survives only as a weak 0.22 fallback when visibility data is missing. **(2) Procedural clouds** — the 4 hardcoded ellipse clusters are replaced by seeded generation: exported `mulberry32` PRNG + `makeCloudSpec(seed, fullness)` build each cloud as 4–9 overlapping ellipses (count/size grow with cloud cover, normalized into a 200×70 viewBox); the seed is the hour timestamp (`floor(ts/3600000) * 31 + slot * 101 + 7`), so each hour of each day has unique clouds, stable within the hour, and scrubbing the timeline morphs the sky to that hour's clouds via a 1.1s `wxFadeIn` crossfade (the previously-unused keyframe). **Tap the clouds to build your own:** each visible cloud handles `pointerdown` and puffs a new blob at the tap x-position — `wxPuff` 0.9s grow-and-fade on an ellipse with `transform-box: fill-box`, max 3 concurrent puffs, cleared on animationend, disabled under reduced motion. **(3) True moon phases** — exported `moonPhase(timestamp)` (synodic model: 29.53058867-day month from the Jan 6 2000 18:14 UTC new-moon epoch → phase 0..1 + illumination fraction + waxing flag), `moonPhaseName(phase)` (8 phase names), and `moonPathD(cx, cy, r, phase)` (terminator path: semicircle limb + half-ellipse terminator, waxing lit right / waning lit left); the night branch now renders the real illuminated fraction over a faint earthshine disc, with glow alpha scaling with illumination), `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` (Open-Meteo fetch gained `visibility` on current + hourly; `HourPoint`/`WeatherData` carry `visibility: number | null`; both scene states (card + modal) pass `visibility` and the active/scrubbed hour's `timestamp` — previewing or scrubbing changes the clouds and moon for that hour; the card content overlay became `pointer-events-none` with `pointer-events-auto` on the °F/°C toggle, day strip, and Details button so clouds are tappable (modal hero overlay likewise); the modal Daylight card gained a dotted-leader **Moon** row — "Waxing Gibbous · 78%" — via the new exports), `src/app/globals.css` (NEW `wxPuff` keyframe + added to the `prefers-reduced-motion` kill block), `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` (+11 tests: moon model at known epoch/full/quarter + 8 phase names, cloud spec determinism/uniqueness/viewBox-bounds/fullness, visibility fog renders at 800m and not at 16km, night scene renders the moon-phase path; mock payload now includes visibility), `scripts/consuela/verify-weather-features.mjs` (NEW — deterministic 11-check Playwright probe with mocked Open-Meteo responses)
- Visual / Motion: The scene now simulates the actual weather instead of a interpretation of it: fog rolls in when real visibility drops (not when humidity guesses say so), every hour has its own hand-built clouds — thicker and wider as cover rises — and tapping a cloud puffs a fresh blob into it where you touched (the (Not Boring) toy, harmless and state-driven). At night the moon is no longer a painted crescent: it shows tonight's true phase — the actual illuminated slice with a faint earthshine disc — and the Daylight card names it ("Full Moon · 100%"). Scrubbing the timeline regenerates that hour's clouds with a soft 1.1s crossfade. All motion is CSS-only and neutralized under `prefers-reduced-motion` (the tap toy included).
- Color sources: unchanged — skin palettes from `WeatherSkins.ts`; cloud blobs keep `skin.cloud`/`skin.cloudDeep`, moon lit path keeps `skin.celestial`.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The weather card now simulates the real weather, not a cartoon of it. Fog appears based on actual visibility distance. The clouds are generated fresh for every hour — thicker when it's cloudier — and you can tap a cloud to puff a new one into it. At night, the moon shows its real phase (tonight's actual illuminated shape), and the details view names it ('Full Moon · 100%')."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-27 — Weather card polish: timeline label collisions fixed, graphs named, Not Boring fidelity pass
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — (1) **Label collision fix:** the day strip and modal scrubber labeled every 3-hour-aligned hour regardless of distance from the NOW marker, so whenever the current hour preceded a multiple-of-3 hour (e.g. 8:30 AM → NOW at 8AM + "9 AM" one step later) the two labels cramped (strip: 26px apart; scrubber: overlapping at 2px). New shared `pickLabelIndices(hours, x, minGap)` builds the label set with a minimum pixel gap (44px strip / 48px scrubber) — time-of-day-proof by construction. (2) **Compact Not Boring ticks:** new `formatHourTick()` renders "9AM"/"12PM" (no space) on strip, scrubber, and hourly chips; the spaced `formatHourLabel` remains for spoken `aria-valuetext`, chip aria-labels, condition lines, and sun-arc times. NOW tick + strip hairline now render in the state accent (was neutral track color). (3) **Graphs named:** tracked-uppercase kickers — "Rest of today" above the card day strip (`skin.inkSoft`), "Next 24 Hours" above the modal scrubber, "Daylight" inside the sun-arc card; hourly section header renamed "Next 24 Hours" → "Hourly" so the phrase isn't duplicated. (4) **Not Boring fidelity:** modal hourly chips lost borders/backgrounds for open columns — active hour marked by a 2.5px accent top bar + 12% accent tint, inactive chips chrome-less; precip % always rendered but `opacity: 0` below 20% (was a stray "·" glyph); chip gap 8→4px, radius 16→12px. Modal hero numerals 54→60px; card "°" 24→28px and modal "°" 20→24px so the degree mark carries big-number presence.
- Visual / Motion: The timelines now read calm and evenly spaced at every hour of the day, and each graph announces itself ("Rest of today", "Next 24 Hours", "Daylight") instead of asking to be guessed. The modal's hourly row is quieter — chrome removed, accent reserved for the selected hour — one step closer to (Not Boring)'s minimal-chrome grammar. No motion changes; no palette changes.
- Color sources: unchanged — skin accents from `WeatherSkins.ts`.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):

- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` (full rewrite — the static seasonal-illustration card with protruding corner icon is replaced by a data-true scene card: centered huge temperature (56–64px SF Pro weight-900, `tracking-[-0.03em]`, tabular-nums, 550ms cubic count-up on any change), condition + H/L + next-hours outlook beneath, and a new bottom **day strip** — rest-of-day temp curve (midpoint-quadratic smoothed SVG path measured to real width via ResizeObserver) with accent rain ticks (≥40% precip, opacity scaled by probability), NOW + 3-hour labels, and press/drag/keyboard (`role="slider"`, arrows + Escape/Home, `aria-valuetext` speaks hour/temp/precip) preview: the hero temp, condition ("7 PM · Clear"), and the whole scene follow the thumb; release animates back to now after 650ms. plus the full Not Boring night skin keyed to real API `is_day`: `#060609→#141420` sky, white ink, one condition accent — storm amber / rain cyan / snow ice / fog gray / default soft red; `cardinalFromDegrees` helper), `src/components/ui/WeatherScene.tsx` (NEW — the data-true scene: five stacked sky layers crossfading via 1.1s opacity transitions, sun disc riding the real sun arc (left/top from sunrise→sunset progress, 0.8s eased) or crescent moon + twinkling stars at night, 4 cloud slots whose count/opacity track `cloud_cover` and drift speed tracks wind, rain streaks (count from precip probability, slant from wind speed/direction) or swaying snow, humidity/fog-code fog banks, lightning flash overlay on storm codes; JS `prefers-reduced-motion` gate freezes all particle motion), `src/components/ui/WeatherParticles.tsx` (NEW — the existing particle system extracted verbatim, now rendered only for active holidays), `src/components/ui/WeatherSeasonArt.tsx` (new opt-out `backdrop` prop — holiday overlays now layer on top of the data-true scene without the old seasonal backdrops), `src/app/globals.css` (NEW `.wx-sky` crossfade class + keyframes `wxCloudDrift`, `wxStarTwinkle`, `wxFogDrift`, `wxRainStreak`, `wxSnowFall`, `wxLightning`, `wxFadeIn`, `wxThumbIn`, `wxHeroIn` + matching `prefers-reduced-motion` kill block), `tests/unit/weather-widget.test.tsx` (rewritten — 7 tests: modal open/close + daily toggle, H/L, strip slider + rain ticks, keyboard preview + return-to-now, night skin via `is_day=0`, modal scrubber valuetext + UV dots), `scripts/consuela/verify-weather-redesign.mjs` (NEW — 30-check Playwright probe), `DESIGN.md` (NEW — dashboard world + weather-card world), `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-08-27-not-boring-weather-redesign-design.md` (direction contract recorded)
- Visual / Motion: The card now reads like (Not Boring) Weather in Consuela's palette — one huge number over a living sky instead of an illustration with an icon in the corner. Every scene element is data-true: clouds thicken as `cloud_cover` rises, rain falls slanted by the real wind, fog banks roll in above 82% humidity, storms flash, and the sun sits where the API says it is on its arc. Day skies are soft pastels (guava in summer) with near-black numerals; after real sunset the card goes full Not Boring — near-black sky, white numerals, a single condition accent. Pressing the day strip previews any hour of the rest of the day: the number counts to that hour's temp, the condition labels the hour, and the sky morphs to match (preview tonight and the card darkens); releasing glides back to now. The modal scrubs the whole next 24 hours the same way — scrub past sunset and the modal's sky darkens too. Skies crossfade (1.1s), temps count up (550ms ease-out), thumbs pop in with a blur-settle; everything is CSS-only and everything stops under `prefers-reduced-motion`. Holidays still overlay the scene (bats, lights, fireworks) with their own particles.

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-26 — Kitchen redesigned: Plan → Shop → Stock
- Added / Changed: `src/app/meals/page.tsx` (rewired to the three-step kitchen — one `SegmentedControl` radiogroup `aria-label="Kitchen"` driving 🍽️ Plan / 🛒 Shop / 🥫 Stock; legacy `?tab=` deep links mapped via the new helper; sync previews wired to the hooks), `src/lib/kitchen-tabs.ts` (NEW — pure legacy tab-param mapping: `grocery`→shop, `pantry`→stock, `recipes`→plan + recipe box, `meals`→plan), `src/components/meals/KitchenFlowCard.tsx` (NEW — shared collapsible stepper card rendered at the top of all three tabs; `div[aria-label="Kitchen flow"]`, active step `aria-current="step"`, collapse persisted to localStorage `consuela-kitchen-flow-collapsed`; mounted/collapse init via `useSyncExternalStore` so the strict `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` rule stays clean), `src/components/meals/SyncPreviewSheet.tsx` (NEW — preview-before-commit Modal: lists exactly what a sync will add, `Add N` confirm, no writes until confirmed), `src/services/mealSync.ts` (+ pure preview methods `previewMealPlanToGrocery` / `previewPantryToGrocery` — no side effects, unit-tested), `src/components/meals/MealsTab.tsx` → `PlanTab.tsx` (rename + flow card + inline Recipe box + ghost-member fix + direct sync button removed), `src/components/meals/RecipesTab.tsx` → `RecipeBox.tsx` (rename — folded into Plan as an expandable box), `src/components/meals/GroceryTab.tsx` → `ShopTab.tsx` (rename + flow card + Buy-again row + single preview-first meal sync + 44px targets), `src/components/meals/PantryTab.tsx` → `StockTab.tsx` (rename + flow card + single preview-first low/out sync + declutter + async add fix), tests: NEW `kitchen-tabs.test.ts`, `kitchen-flow-card.test.tsx`, `meal-sync-preview.test.ts`, `sync-preview-sheet.test.tsx`; `grocery-handoff.test.tsx` + `grocery-override-ui.test.tsx` re-pointed at ShopTab
- Visual / Motion: The Meals page is now one calm loop instead of four parallel tabs. Every step opens with the same collapsible Plan → Shop → Stock stepper card showing where you are (`aria-current` on the active step); tap it to collapse once you know the way. Plan keeps the weekly grid with the Recipe box folded inline. Shop keeps the single list, check-off, 📌 pins, Clear, and the "Send N to pantry" bulk bar + Undo, and adds a Buy-again row. Stock keeps the pantry grid + Cook with what you have. Both syncs are now preview-first: tapping "Add missing from meal plan" (Shop) or "Add low & out to grocery list" (Stock) opens a sheet listing exactly what will be added — nothing touches the list until you tap Add N. All motion is the existing glass-settles language; `prefers-reduced-motion` untouched.
- Color sources: Existing warm-glass + accent tokens only; stepper uses the existing accent for the active step. No new palette entries.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + §1.1 nav table + §1.5 journeys + Change Log. Known pre-existing concerns surfaced during verification (NOT plan regressions — baseline-verified): (1) CapsuleNav can overlap inline-Modal footers because PageShell `<main class="relative z-10">` creates a stacking context that traps `z-[80]` modals under the `z-50` nav (RecipeModal portals to body and is unaffected); (2) grocery items with category `other` render nowhere in ShopTab (`groceryCategories` has no `other` entry — same hole in baseline); (3) `/meals` logs one hydration mismatch per guest load — server SSR uses the PB-warmed members cache while the guest client hydrates with fallbacks in the PlanTab member strip (baseline MealsTab had the identical pre-mount `db.selectMembers()` pattern).
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The Meals screen is now one simple loop: Plan your week, Shop what's missing, Stock what you bought. A little stepper card at the top always shows which step you're on, and you can collapse it once you know the way. Recipes now live inside Plan as a fold-out box. And nothing lands on your grocery list by surprise — both 'add missing meal ingredients' and 'add low pantry items' show you a preview first, and only add what you confirm."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-26 — Google Calendar sync month-navigation bug fixed (month-aware events)
- Added / Changed: NEW `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts` (pure, unit-tested: `parseGoogleStart` — all-day date-only strings parsed as local dates so US timezones don't shift the day back one; `mapGoogleEvent` — Google row → CalEvent keeping day + month + year, id now `g_{google_id}_{day}_{month}_{year}_{time}`; `eventInMonth` — events without month/year repeat every month, dated events match only their own month/year), NEW `tests/unit/calendar/google-mapping.test.ts` (9 tests), `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` (`CalEvent` gained optional `month`/`year`; `syncGoogleEvents` rewritten to use `mapGoogleEvent` and NO LONGER filters events to the current month — the backend serves a 30-days-back / 90-days-forward window and all of it is kept; dedupe key now includes month/year; `dayEventMap`, `selectedEvents`, `upcomingByDay` filter through `eventInMonth(e, month, year)` so the grid dots, Today panel, and Upcoming panel only show each event in its own month)
- Visual / Motion: No styling change. Behavior fix: before, Google events were mapped to day-of-month only and filtered to the current month at sync time — so (1) navigating to next month showed none of the synced Google events that exist there, and (2) this month's Google events ghosted onto the same day numbers of every other month. Now each Google event appears exactly in its own month; family-created events (no month/year) keep their existing repeats-every-month behavior. Backend (`src/lib/google/calendar.ts` + `/api/google-calendar`) audited and unchanged — it already stores real `start_iso` dates across the 30d-back/90d-forward window.
- Color sources: None — logic-only change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + Change Log. Note: old localStorage Google events (pre-fix id format, no month) still repeat every month until the next sync replaces them — one auto-sync on mount clears them (member==="Google" rows are dropped and re-mapped).
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The Google Calendar sync now handles months correctly. Before, synced events were stamped with just a day number, so they'd copy themselves onto the same day of every month, and events in other months never appeared when you navigated to them. Now each Google event carries its real date and shows up only in its own month — flip to next month and its events are there; flip back and this month's events stay put. Your hand-added family events still repeat every month like before."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-26 — Console hygiene: theme script via next/script + Calendar hydration mismatch fixed
- Added / Changed: `src/app/layout.tsx` (anti-FOUC theme-init script moved from raw `<script dangerouslySetInnerHTML>` — which React 19 warns about during client rendering — to `next/script` with `strategy="beforeInteractive"`, the Next.js 16 app-router-supported pattern that injects the inline script into the server HTML head and runs it before hydration), `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` (NEW `mounted` flag via `useSyncExternalStore(subscribeNoop, clientTrue, serverFalse)` — server snapshot false, client snapshot true, no effect/setState so the strict `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` rule stays clean; NEW `renderEvents`/`renderSchedules` memos that serve the deterministic fallbacks (`events` / `getInitialSchedules()`) until mount and the localStorage-seeded state after; `dayEventMap`, `selectedEvents`, `upcomingByDay`, `sortedSchedules`, and the routines count/empty-state render sites now read the gated values — state, CRUD handlers, and the localStorage write-back effects still use the real state)
- Visual / Motion: No visual change. Fixes two dev-console errors: (1) "Encountered a script tag while rendering React component" at RootLayout, and (2) "Hydration failed because the server rendered HTML didn't match the client" in the month-grid day dots — root cause was `calEvents`/`schedules` lazy-initializing from localStorage on the client (stored events) while the server rendered the fallback data, so dot counts and event rows differed at hydration. Theme behavior unchanged: data-theme is still set pre-hydration (verified at domcontentloaded in dark and light). Stored events/schedules appear one frame after mount instead of at hydration — no flash because server HTML and first client render are identical fallbacks.
- Color sources: None — no color changes.
- Agent action required: Update this section + Change Log. Convention: localStorage-seeded render data must be mounted-gated (this page now uses the useSyncExternalStore variant because the strict set-state-in-effect rule flags the classic `useEffect(() => setMounted(true))` when the flag feeds memo cascades).
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "Two console errors are gone. The theme script now uses Next.js's official pre-hydration script slot (same behavior — no theme flash), and the calendar no longer mismatches between server and browser when you have saved events: the grid renders the default data for the first paint and swaps in your saved events immediately after."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-26 — Motion system: "glass settles" (full-dashboard motion dive)
- Added / Changed: `src/app/globals.css` (NEW ease tokens `--ease-spring: cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1)` + `--ease-settle: cubic-bezier(0.16,1,0.3,1)` in `@theme`; all 16 ad-hoc spring-bezier spellings in globals.css migrated to `var(--ease-spring)`; NEW keyframes `pageSettle` (opacity 0→1 + translateY 10px→0 + blur 6px→0, 240ms settle), `panelSwap` (fade + 6px rise, 200ms), `modalExit` (scale .97 + 10px drop + fade, 150ms), `overlayExit`, `toastExit` (reverse slide, 180ms); NEW utilities `.page-settle` / `.panel-swap`, both added to the `prefers-reduced-motion` kill block), `src/components/ui/PageShell.tsx` (now a client component — `usePathname()` keys `<main>` so every route change remounts and replays the 240ms settle; chat page got the class on its own shell), `src/components/ui/Modal.tsx` (REAL exit animation: internal `closed/open/closing` phase machine — on `open`→false it plays modalExit + overlayExit for 150ms then unmounts; reduced-motion unmounts instantly; enter now `0.35s var(--ease-spring)` via inline style), `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx` (the old close path froze the toast for 200ms then popped it — now plays `toastExit` 180ms inside that window; reduced-motion instant), `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` + `src/app/meals/page.tsx` + `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` + `src/app/ha/page.tsx` (tab panels wrapped in `key={tab}` `.panel-swap` divs — Calendar⇄Schedule, Meals/Grocery/Pantry/Recipes, Tasks⇄Leaderboard, and all five HA tabs now cross-fade with a 6px rise instead of hard-swapping), `src/app/chat/page.tsx` (page-settle on its custom shell), `src/app/page.tsx` (MemberPickerModal now rendered unconditionally with `open={pickerOpen}` so its exit can play — Modal owns its mount lifecycle), `src/components/leaderboard/ShareCard.tsx` (removed `if (!open) return null` guard — it defeated the exit), `src/components/suggestions/SuggestionPinModal.tsx` (guard replaced with a `session` counter that remounts `PinModalInner` per open — keeps the fresh-input semantics AND plays the exit; `open` is now threaded into PinModalInner, which previously resolved a bare `open` identifier to `window.open` (always truthy) — latent bug the old guard masked).
- Visual / Motion: The dashboard now has one coherent motion language — "glass settles": every route arrival fades in with a 10px rise and a 6px blur-out over 240ms (the frosted-glass signature), every tab panel swap is a quick 200ms fade-and-rise, modals exit with a 150ms settle-down (faster than their 350ms spring entrance), and toasts slide back up 180ms when dismissed instead of freezing then vanishing. The capsule nav pill keeps the only spring curve in the app; everything else uses decelerating settles. No new loops, no scroll-driven effects, no page-load choreography — all new motion is one-shot transform/opacity/filter, and every piece is neutralized under `prefers-reduced-motion` (modals/toasts close instantly).
- Color sources: None — motion-only change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + §1.3 + Change Log. Motion convention going forward: use `--ease-spring` for the one playful signature (nav pill) and `--ease-settle` for all arrivals; exits are 150–180ms; don't add new infinite loops outside the weather/chat ambient systems.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The whole dashboard got a motion pass. Pages now glide in with a soft glass-focus effect when you switch tabs instead of hard-swapping, the panels inside Calendar, Meals, Tasks, and House fade gently when you switch views, pop-up windows settle away smoothly instead of vanishing, and notification banners slide off instead of freezing. Everything respects the reduced-motion setting — with it on, everything is instant."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-18 — Settings Layout & display: reorder arrows work with real clicks (drag scoped to the ⋮⋮ handle)
- Added / Changed: `src/app/settings/page.tsx` — the Visible-group widget rows were whole-row `draggable` (`draggable` + `onDragStart={handleDragStart(widget.id)}` on the row `<div>`). Any mousedown + micro-movement on a child control (the ↑/↓ arrows, the visibility toggles) started an HTML5 drag on the row, which cancelled the child's click — so the arrows appeared dead in real use (human clicks almost always include 2–5px of movement; only pixel-perfect programmatic clicks worked). Fix: `draggable` + `onDragStart` moved from the row `<div>` onto the ⋮⋮ handle `<span>` (the element with `cursor-grab` / "Drag to reorder" tooltip). `onDragOver` / `onDragLeave` / `onDrop` / `onDragEnd` stay on the row (`dragend` bubbles up from the handle, so the drop-target highlight + drag-end cleanup are unchanged).
- Visual / Motion: No visual change — arrows, toggles, and drag all look identical; only the click behavior is fixed. Verified via Playwright: (1) plain click on ↑/↓ reorders; (2) a jittered click (mousedown + 4px move + mouseup) now also reorders — before the fix the event log showed `mousedown: BUTTON → dragstart: DIV` with no `click` ever firing; (3) drag from the ⋮⋮ handle still reorders (event log: `dragstart:SPAN → dragenter/dragover → drop:SPAN → dragend:SPAN`; Morning Briefing dragged to position 3 correctly).
- Color sources: None — pure interaction change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The up/down reorder arrows in Settings → Layout & display now work reliably. Before, the entire widget row was draggable, so a tiny mouse wiggle while clicking an arrow turned the click into a drag and the arrow did nothing. Dragging still works — from the ⋮⋮ handle."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-18 — Kitchen "orb" true root cause fixed (glass ::before position containment)
- Added / Changed: `src/app/globals.css` — added `position: relative` to the three Apple-material base classes (`.material-thin, .glass-subtle`, `.material-regular, .glass`, `.material-thick, .glass-strong`). Also calmed the specular/glow values while investigating (`.glass::before` white 0.05→0.02, `.liquid-glass::before` 0.06→0.02 in both `globals.css` and the dead `src/styles/materials.css`, `.widget-card` outer glow 40px@22%→20px@12% dark / 48px@28%→24px@14% hover / 32px@16%→16px@8% light).
- Visual / Motion: The persistent top-left "orb" on the Meals and Pantry tabs was NOT the accent glow shadows (removed 2026-08-14) nor the specular opacity. Root cause (found via Playwright DOM inspection): `.glass` elements are `position: static`, but their `::before` specular layer is `position: absolute; inset: 0`. With no positioned ancestor on the card, `inset: 0` resolved against the nearest positioned ancestor — `<main class="relative z-10">` (the full 1440×1810 page) — so every `.glass` card painted a giant `radial-gradient(120% 60% at 20% 0%)` glow across the entire top-left of the page. Meals/Pantry have `.glass` cards near the top (stat tiles, meal/pantry cards); Grocery's top cards are `.widget-card` (no `::before`), which is why Grocery always looked clean. Adding `position: relative` to the material classes contains each `::before` inside its own card, restoring the intended subtle top sheen and removing the page-wide orb. Verified via Playwright screenshots: Meals + Pantry top-left now flat dark, identical to Grocery.
- Color sources: None new — the specular sheen values are unchanged in hue; only containment (position) and a calm-down of opacity/spread.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The soft glow of light in the top-left of the Kitchen (Meals and Pantry) screens is finally gone. It turned out each frosted glass card was accidentally painting its highlight across the whole page instead of just inside itself — a one-line CSS fix (positioning) now keeps every card's sheen inside the card, so the background stays clean and dark, matching the Grocery tab."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-11 — Three-mode Home layouts: phone / tablet / desktop
- Added / Changed: `src/lib/layout-config.ts` — `LayoutMode` = `"phone" | "tablet" | "desktop"`; new threshold constants `PHONE_MAX_WIDTH = 700` (catches iPad mini 744/768; no phone is ≥700px portrait) and `TABLET_MAX_WIDTH = 1280` (covers iPad Pro 12.9″ = 1024, Nest Hub = 1024); new pure `computeLayoutMode(isPortrait, width)` (unit-tested): portrait <700 → phone, portrait 700–1279 → tablet, landscape OR ≥1280 → desktop; `HomeLayoutConfig` shape → `{ phone: { widgets }, tablet: { widgets }, desktop: { widgets } }`; new `TABLET_COL_SPANS` (morningBriefing / weather / consuelaSuggestions `col-span-2`, everything else 1) + `toTabletOrder` (hole-free full-width-first partition of a single-column order); `homeGridClass` / `widgetSpanClass` / `homeFooterSpanClass` now take a `LayoutMode` — tablet = `"grid grid-cols-2 gap-6 auto-rows-min"` + `col-span-N` (+ footer `col-span-2`), phone = `grid-cols-1` + `""`, desktop = the existing filmstrip classes; `loadLayoutConfig` migration — v1 `{ widgets }` maps into ALL three buckets, v2 `{ portrait, landscape }` maps phone=portrait / desktop=landscape / tablet=partitioned portrait, v3 (app-written) round-trips exactly with id-validation only (no self-heal reorder on the v3 path); plain JSON via `saveLayoutConfig`, no version constant. `src/hooks/useOrientation.tsx` — now returns `{ orientation: LayoutMode; mounted }`, SSR defaults to desktop, listeners on the portrait media query + resize. `src/hooks/useHomeLayout.tsx` — mode-aware `widgetsFor(mode)` lists + mutators; the old `Orientation` type name is fully removed. `src/app/page.tsx` — renders the live mode's grid via the mode-aware helpers; pre-mount `HOME_GRID_FALLBACK` (`grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3`) keeps the SSR frame stable. `src/app/settings/page.tsx` — "Layout & display" gains 📱 Phone / 📱 Tablet / 🖥️ Desktop `SegmentedControl` tabs; reset toast now reads "phone, tablet, and desktop"; export JSON includes all three buckets. `tests/unit/layout-config.test.ts` — rewritten 3-mode contract (23 tests; replaces the 4 stale failures left by the 2026-08-07 filmstrip change).
- Visual / Motion: A tablet held in portrait (700–1279px wide — iPad mini, iPad Pro 12.9″, Nest Hub) no longer shows the tall single-column phone feed: it's now a **2-column pastel bento** — the morning briefing, weather, and Consuela's suggestions each stretch across the full row (col-span-2) and the remaining cards pair up beneath them with no holes; the This Week footer spans both columns. The tablet order is derived from the phone order (full-width widgets keep their relative order first, then the rest pair up), so a user's phone arrangement carries over automatically. Phones (portrait <700px) keep the familiar single-column vertical stack and desktop (landscape, or portrait ≥1280px) keeps the horizontal filmstrip — both unchanged. No new motion; reduced-motion untouched.
- Color sources: None — pure layout change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):

On a tablet held upright the widgets arrange themselves into a tidy two-column grid: the morning briefing, weather, and Consuela's suggestions stretch across the full row with the rest paired up underneath, and your phone order carries over automatically. Desktop keeps the sideways filmstrip and phones keep scrolling vertically, exactly as before. In Settings → Layout & display you can switch between Phone, Tablet, and Desktop tabs to reorder each layout independently, and Reset restores all three."

- Added / Changed: `src/app/globals.css` (.widget-card scoped text tokens + `--widget-accent`/`--widget-accent-strong` + `.widget-accent-text` + `.schedule-row` scrim), `src/components/ui/ScheduleDisplay.tsx` (rows get `schedule-row` scrim class, tint moved to inline `backgroundImage`, time text `text-text-secondary`→`text-text-primary`), `src/components/patterns/DayStrip.tsx` (active day uses `--color-accent-button` fill/border/shadow instead of accent-selected), `src/components/ui/Chip.tsx` (accent tone text → `widget-accent-text`; selected fill → `--color-accent-button`), `src/components/suggestions/HomeSuggestionsWidget.tsx` + `HomeLeaderboardWidget.tsx` + `MorningBriefingWidget.tsx` + `src/app/page.tsx` (accent-colored action links/badge/arrow → `widget-accent-text`), `src/components/meals/CurrentMealWidget.tsx` (time badge + tag colors → `var(--widget-accent-strong, accent)`), padding standardization (`SectionCard` footer `p-4`→`p-5`; `ScheduleDisplay` header drops `pb-4`; `CurrentMealWidget` content `p-5 pt-4 pl-14`→`p-5 pl-14`; `StatTile` `px-4 py-3`→`p-4`).

- Added / Changed: `src/components/patterns/WidgetCard.tsx` (new — tone-driven card shell: `--widget-tone` CSS var injected inline; 160° pastel gradient over the warm-glass base, `rounded-3xl`, tone-tinted border, soft outer glow `0 0 40px tone@22%`, inset top highlight, protruding top-left 56px icon with radial halo `blur(8px)` + `weatherGlowPulse 7s` + drop shadow), `src/app/globals.css` (`.widget-card` + light-mode `:root[data-theme="light"]` / `:root:not([data-theme="dark"])` overrides via `color-mix`, `[data-contrast="boost"]` border bump), `src/components/patterns/SectionCard.tsx` (now renders `WidgetCard`; icon moves from the inner 40px badge to the protruding slot; header `p-5 pl-14`; new `tone` prop; default = app accent), `src/lib/layout-config.ts` (`WIDGET_SPANS` desktop span map), `src/app/page.tsx` (Home restructured into `grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 auto-rows-min`; weather `lg:col-span-3` unchanged, suggestions `lg:col-span-2`, rest span 1; `mt-3`/`mt-4` spacers became `lg:mt-0`; This Week full-width below the grid; aiQuickAsk now a violet `WidgetCard` with protruding Icon3D; stat tiles row kept `grid-cols-3`), `src/components/patterns/StatTile.tsx` (WidgetCard shell, tone hexes accent-var/mint/amber/rose, `lg:aspect-square` centered), `src/components/ui/ScheduleDisplay.tsx` (gains its first proper card shell — ice blue `#22d3ee`, protruding 🕐, header row with "N upcoming"; liquid-glass rows untouched), `src/components/meals/CurrentMealWidget.tsx` (seasonal gradient shell → mint `#10b981` WidgetCard with protruding 🍽️; floating seasonal emoji + inner accents kept), `src/components/briefing/MorningBriefingWidget.tsx` (sunset `#f97316`; acknowledged state now a WidgetCard), `src/components/leaderboard/HomeLeaderboardWidget.tsx` (amber `#f59e0b`), `src/components/suggestions/HomeSuggestionsWidget.tsx` (violet `#8b5cf6`).
- Visual / Motion: The Home dashboard now reads as one design family. Every widget card wears a soft pastel wash in its own identity color (briefing sunset orange, quick-ask/suggestions violet, leaderboard amber, today cool blue, schedule ice blue, current meal/this-week mint, tasks soft rose) over the dark glass, with a glowing tone-tinted border, a faint outer glow, and its emoji popping out of the top-left corner on a slow-pulsing halo — the same language as the weather card. On desktop the widgets flow into a 3-column bento (weather hero spans the full row; suggestions spans two columns; the stat tiles become three square pastel tiles) while mobile keeps the familiar single-column stack. Weather card itself is visually unchanged.

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-06 — Weather widget: protruding top-left icon, larger card, slower motion
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx` — two follow-ups to the protruding-icon change: (1) **bigger widget**: card `minHeight` 180→220px, icon 88→96px (scale 1.2222→1.3333), frosted panel `p-4 pl-[72px]` → `p-5 pl-[80px]`, main temp `text-[52px]`→`text-[60px]` with `text-3xl` degree glyph, condition text `text-sm`→`text-base`, feels-like `text-[11px]`→`text-xs`, location row `text-xs`→`text-sm`, °F/°C pill `text-[11px] px-2.5 py-0.5`→`text-xs px-3 py-1`, pre-mount ⛅ fallback 88→96px / `text-6xl`→`text-7xl`; (2) **slower motion (~2×)**: protruding halo `weatherGlowPulse 3.5s`→`7s`; entrance `weatherCardEnter 0.65s`→`1s`; temp pop `0.45s`→`0.7s`; forecast slide `0.38s` (stagger 0.06s)→`0.55s` (0.08s); expand `duration-500`→`duration-700`; icon internals — sun night glow 4→8s, spin 20→32s, star pulses 2/3/2.5→4/6/5s; sun day glow 3.5→7s, spin 14→28s, rays `weatherRayPulse 2.2s` (0.28s stagger)→`4.4s` (0.45s), core glow 2.2→4.4s; partly-cloudy moon 5→10s, sun rays 4→8s, cloud bob 5→10s; cloudy bobs 7s/1.8s→12s/3.6s and 5.5→11s; rainy cloud bob 4.5→9s, `weatherRainDrop 1.35s`→`2.2s` with delays scaled ~1.5×; snowy cloud bob 5→9s, `weatherSnowDrift 2.4s`→`4.5s` with delays ~2×.
- Visual / Motion: The weather icon still pops out of the top-left corner (24px above / 24px left), now at 96px, sitting on a slower, calmer accent glow. The whole card is noticeably bigger — taller shell, larger temperature, larger type — and every animation (icon spin/bob/fall, halo pulse, entrance, expand, forecast stagger) runs at roughly half its previous speed so the widget reads as calm and ambient rather than busy.

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-07 — Landscape filmstrip (scroll left↔right instead of down)
- Added / Changed: `src/lib/layout-config.ts` — new `WIDGET_WIDTHS: Record<WidgetId, string>` map (hero `w-[720px]` for weather + This Week footer, `w-[480px]` suggestions, `w-[420px]` current meal, `w-[360px]` the rest); `homeGridClass("landscape")` changed from `"grid grid-cols-3 gap-6 auto-rows-min"` (vertical-flow 3-col bento) to `"flex gap-6 overflow-x-auto pb-4 pt-6 items-start snap-x snap-proximity"` (horizontal filmstrip); `widgetSpanClass` returns `shrink-0 snap-start ${WIDGET_WIDTHS[id]}` in landscape (was `col-span-N`); `homeFooterSpanClass` returns `shrink-0 snap-start w-[720px]` in landscape (was `col-span-3`). Portrait (`"grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 auto-rows-min"` + `""` spans) unchanged — single-column vertical stack. `HOME_GRID_FALLBACK` + `WIDGET_SPANS` retained for the SSR/pre-mount frame. `page.tsx` unchanged (already consumes `gridClass` / `widgetSpanClass` / `homeFooterSpanClass`).
- Visual / Motion: On a wide screen (landscape orientation) the Home dashboard no longer flows vertically as a 3-column bento you scroll DOWN through. It's now one horizontal filmstrip: each widget is a fixed-width column (360–720px) sitting side-by-side, extending off the right edge of the viewport, so you scroll/swipe LEFT↔RIGHT to reveal the next widget. Gentle `snap-proximity` + `snap-start` lets each card settle to the left edge as you scroll past it. `pt-6` padding keeps each card's protruding top-left icon (which sits ~22px above the card) from being clipped by the horizontal scroll container's implicit `overflow-y: auto`. Portrait (phone) is unchanged — widgets stack vertically and you scroll up/down as before. The This Week card becomes the last filmstrip item (~720px wide) instead of a full-width footer row. No new motion keyframes; reduced-motion untouched (snap is a scroll behavior, not animation).
- Color sources: None — pure layout change.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "On your computer (or anything in landscape), the Home dashboard now scrolls sideways. Instead of stacking the widgets into a tall 3-column grid you scroll down through, they sit side-by-side as a filmstrip and you swipe left and right to move between them — each card snaps gently to the left edge as you pass it. On your phone in portrait nothing's changed; you still scroll up and down."

### UI Change Record — 2026-08-06 — CapsuleNav: expanding neon-lime pill navigation replaces BottomNav
- Added / Changed: `src/components/ui/CapsuleNav.tsx` (new), `src/components/ui/PageShell.tsx` (renders CapsuleNav), `src/app/chat/page.tsx` (renders CapsuleNav), `src/app/globals.css` (`.capsule-nav` tokens + reduced-motion guard), `src/components/ui/BottomNav.tsx` (deleted)
- Visual / Motion: The persistent bottom tab bar is now a **floating bottom-center glass capsule**: dark charcoal tint (`rgba(8,10,12,0.60)` dark / `rgba(0,0,0,0.16)` light, same `data-theme` MutationObserver as the old nav), 24px blur + saturate, `rounded-full`, soft drop shadow (`0 24px 48px -12px rgba(0,0,0,0.55)`) and a bright 1px inner top highlight. The six items (Home, Ask, Meals, Tasks, Settings, More — same routes, same inline SVGs, strokeWidth 2→2.5 on active) sit as small dark circular buttons (`bg-white/[0.06]`, inset shadow, gray/white icons). The **active item expands into a large rounded pill**: width animates 0fr→1fr via a CSS grid-column transition (`0.38s cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)`), the pill gets a lime-tinted glass fill + `rgba(120,240,90,0.38)` border + outer glow, the icon area becomes a solid **neon-lime circle (`--capsule-accent: #78f05a`)** with a white icon, and the label slides in on the right (`0.3s` spring `cubic-bezier(0.34,1.56,0.64,1)`, 60ms delay, fade + translate). Inactive icons sit centered in their dark circles; hovering brightens them. On narrow viewports the whole capsule scales down via `--capsule-scale: min(1, (100vw - 1.5rem) / 500px)` so the 6-item dock always fits (fixed element, no page overflow). `prefers-reduced-motion` zeroes all capsule transitions and shows the active label instantly. `.tap-sm` press/hover/focus contract preserved; each item is a `<button>` with `aria-label` + `aria-current="page"`, `router.prefetch` on hover, `pb-safe` respected.
- Color sources: `--capsule-accent: #78f05a` (dedicated var — one-line override point for future Accent Studio integration); all surfaces use the existing Warm Glass rgba/white-alpha language from BottomNav.
- Agent action required: Update this section + "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + §1.1 + Change Log.
- User-facing description (copy-paste ready for responses):
  > "The bottom navigation is now a floating glass capsule. The tab you're on expands into a bright neon-lime pill — the icon sits in a solid green circle with the label sliding in beside it — while the other tabs stay as quiet dark circles. Switching tabs feels smooth and springy, and it shrinks itself slightly on small phones so it always fits."
