- 2026-08-26 — fix(calendar): Google-synced events now month-accurate (no ghost events, no dropped months). Root cause: `syncGoogleEvents` in `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` mapped Google events to day-of-month only (`day: dayNum`) AND filtered them to the current month at sync time, while the grid/panels keyed events by day number for every displayed month — so current-month Google events ghosted onto the same day numbers in all other months, and next-month events never appeared when navigated to. Backend audited clean (`src/lib/google/calendar.ts` stores real `start_iso` across a 30d-back/90d-forward window; `/api/google-calendar` returns all cached rows). Fix: NEW `src/lib/calendar/google-mapping.ts` — `parseGoogleStart` (all-day date-only parsed as local dates, preserving the existing US-timezone shift fix), `mapGoogleEvent` (keeps day+month+year; id `g_{google_id}_{day}_{month}_{year}_{time}`), `eventInMonth` (no month/year = repeats every month, preserving family-event behavior). `CalEvent` gained optional month/year; sync keeps the full window (no current-month filter), dedupe includes month/year; `dayEventMap`/`selectedEvents`/`upcomingByDay` filter via `eventInMonth`. Tests: NEW `tests/unit/calendar/google-mapping.test.ts` (9, TDD red→green). Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect; suite 569/569; Playwright probe with mocked sync (Aug 12/28 + Sep 3/12 events) — August shows 12+28, September shows 3+12 with day 28 clean, July shows no Google ghosts, family repeats intact, no page errors.
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): Calendar ⇄ Schedule tabs — solid accent active pill. `src/app/globals.css` — `.calendar-tab.is-active` was a 24% accent wash + primary text that blended into the glass track (hard to tell which tab was highlighted); now a solid accent gradient (`--color-accent-button` → 76% accent mixed with `#111827`), white text, inset top highlight, accent glow shadow via `color-mix`; added `.calendar-tab.is-active:hover` so hover doesn't dim the white label; inactive tabs unchanged (muted text, transparent). Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect; suite 560/560; detector 8 unchanged; Playwright confirms solid gradient + white text in dark and light and the active pill follows the tap.
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): selected-today keeps both signals — clear translucent box + today circle. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — day-cell className was an either/or ternary (`is-selected` XOR `is-today`), so today's marker vanished whenever today was selected; now independent flags (`is-selected` AND `is-today`). `src/app/globals.css` — today circle un-scoped from `:not(.is-selected)` (always renders on today); selected today gains a 2px white ring on the circle; new `.calendar-day-btn.is-selected.is-today` swaps the dense gradient for a clear translucent accent box (20% fill dark / 22% light via `color-mix`, 60%+white accent border, inset white ring, softer glow, scale 1.06 kept) with matching overrides in both light blocks (`:root[data-theme="light"] .is-today` outranks the base rule); event dots in selected-today use accent color to stay visible on the tint. Verified: typecheck clean; eslint only the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect; suite 560/560; detector 8 unchanged; Playwright confirms both classes + solid circle + translucent fill (no gradient) when today is selected, and circle-on-today + gradient-on-selection when another day is selected, in dark and light.
- 2026-08-26 — fix(ui): console hygiene — theme script via next/script + Calendar hydration mismatch fixed. `src/app/layout.tsx` — anti-FOUC theme-init script moved from raw `<script dangerouslySetInnerHTML>` (React 19 dev warning "Encountered a script tag while rendering React component") to `next/script strategy="beforeInteractive"` (Next.js 16 app-router pattern: injected into server HTML head, runs pre-hydration; verified data-theme set at domcontentloaded in dark + light). `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — fixed "Hydration failed… server rendered HTML didn't match the client" in the month-grid day dots: `calEvents`/`schedules` lazy-init from localStorage on the client (stored events) while the server rendered fallbacks, so dot counts/event rows differed at hydration. Added a `mounted` flag via `useSyncExternalStore(subscribeNoop, clientTrue, serverFalse)` (no effect/setState — the strict `react-hooks/set-state-in-effect` rule flags the classic mounted-effect when the flag feeds memo cascades) + `renderEvents`/`renderSchedules` memos serving deterministic fallbacks until mount; `dayEventMap`/`selectedEvents`/`upcomingByDay`/`sortedSchedules` + routines count/empty-state render sites read the gated values; state/CRUD/write-back effects unchanged. Verified: typecheck clean; eslint back to the 1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect only; suite 560/560; Playwright probes — zero hydration/script-tag console errors in dark + light, including a repro with stored events differing from fallbacks (dots render correctly after mount); detector findings unchanged (8 pre-existing).
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): Calendar month grid adopts Apple Calendar signatures. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — grid header restructured: month title left + `.calendar-month-nav` group (prev chevron ‹ Today pill › next chevron); today's date number gains `is-today-number`. `src/app/globals.css` — today's number renders as a 23px filled accent circle (white numeral + accent-matched glow, scoped `:not(.is-selected)`); `.is-today` tile tint softened (14%→8% dark / 12%→7% light) and re-derived from `--color-accent-selected` via `color-mix` (was `--calendar-accent-rgb` rgba that could mismatch the circle under non-blue accents); `.calendar-month-title` 1.05rem/800 → 1.3rem/600 with muted year; `.calendar-today-btn` full 999px pill; new ghost chevrons (borderless round 36px, dark + light hover); `.calendar-day-dot` 3.2px → 4.2px. Verified: typecheck clean; 0 new lint (1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect unchanged); suite 560/560; Playwright probes confirm circle (23.2px, 999px radius, accent fill, matching glow) + nav order + pill radius + dot size in dark and light, no page errors, no overflow.
- 2026-08-26 — feat(ui): Calendar page polished onto the Home widget glass system. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — hero, month grid, Today panel, Upcoming panel, Family Routines panel, both Add/Edit form cards, and the upcoming day cards migrated from `Card`/one-off glass to the shared `WidgetCard` shell (`src/components/patterns/WidgetCard.tsx`) with Home-palette tones: grid `#3b82f6`, Today + Routines panels `#22d3ee`, Upcoming `#10b981`, forms `#8b5cf6`, hero = app accent; routine cards gained inline `--routine-color`; member chips dropped inline active-gradient overrides. `src/app/globals.css` — `.calendar-hero-card`/`.calendar-panel`/`.calendar-upcoming-card` reduced to layout-only (glass shell now inherited from `.widget-card`, incl. hover resonance, light mode, contrast boost); `.calendar-event-card` + `.calendar-routine-card` frosted with their own colors via `color-mix` (dark + light); `.calendar-member-chip.is-active` tints from `--chip-color`; member chips now render the shared `Avatar` (`variant="emoji"`, `size="xs"` — same member-color circle + tone ring as the Home family strip; deleted the 21.6px `.calendar-member-avatar` dot the 32px emoji overflowed); `.calendar-sync-btn` hardcoded cyan → accent; `.calendar-day-btn` unselected cells now visible glass tiles (tone-tinted fill + hairline tone border + inset highlight, dark + light scoped `:not(.is-selected):not(.is-today)`), Today + selected states given crisper borders. Verified: Home `.widget-card` vs Calendar grid card computed styles byte-identical in dark and light; typecheck clean; 0 new lint (1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect unchanged); suite 560/560; Playwright probes clean (no page errors, no horizontal overflow at 390/1440, both tabs + open form).
- 2026-08-26 — fix(backend): audit follow-up — regression tests for input route guards + dead demo cleanup. Added `tests/unit/input-route-guards.test.ts` (5 tests: OCR/voice/photo multipart guards, email-forward validation, email-forward `members` lookup + missing `consuela_saved_locations` fallback); red-green verified by stashing the route fixes (4 failures) and restoring them (5 passes). Deleted dead phantom-collection demo modules: `src/lib/family-ai.ts`, `src/lib/hermes-tools-enhanced.ts`, `src/lib/morning-briefing.ts`, old `src/hooks/useMorningBriefing.ts`, `src/components/morning-briefing/`, `src/components/family-ai/`. Fixed `src/db/features/seed.ts` `users`→`members`. Verified suite 560/560, `tsc` clean, eslint clean on touched files, live route probes unchanged.
- 2026-08-26 — fix(backend): comprehensive backend audit — gateway unblocked (autodate fields), unauthenticated-PB reads fixed, dead/dangerous routes removed. Full sweep of all 78 API routes (safe probes: empty bodies / missing auth / fake ids; side-effect routes avoided) surfaced a second class of breakage. **(1) Gateway unblocked — the biggest win:** NO PB collection had the standard `created`/`updated` autodate fields, but the sessioned gateway `/api/db/[collection]` defaults to `sort=-created`, so EVERY browser-side gateway read 400'd at PB ("Something went wrong…") → bare empty-body 500 → the dual-mode client silently fell back to stale local cache (cross-device sync dead for all 19 allowlisted collections). Fix in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts`: new `withAutodate()` injects `created`/`updated` autodate fields into every collection and a shared `buildField()` now drives both the create and schema-patch paths (autodate emits `onCreate`/`onUpdate`, no `required`); re-ran `npm run pb:seed` → all collections patched +2 fields. Gateway routes hardened to return parseable JSON instead of empty 500s: `src/app/api/db/[collection]/route.ts` + `[id]/route.ts` wrap PB calls in try/catch → `db_error` 502, and bad JSON bodies → `invalid_json` 400. **(2) Unauthenticated-PB reads fixed:** several server libs still used the public `getPB()` client, which 403s on every locked collection. New `getAuthedPB()` in `src/lib/pb-auth.ts` (same cached admin client + token refresh as `withAdmin`) for call sites that read more naturally with a `pb` handle. Switched: `skill-tree.ts` (GET /api/skill-tree was 500 "Failed to load skill tree data" → now 200, creates/reads real profiles), `schedule-analytics.ts` (was 500 "Only superusers can perform this action." → now 200), `recurring-patterns.ts` (collections already seeded → now returns real data), `family-memory.ts` (its `consuela_family_memories` collection was never seeded → added as the 37th collection in `pb-seed.ts`, now create/read/delete round-trips). **(3) formData + email routes:** `ocr/extract`, `voice/process`, `photo/process` threw Next's raw 500 on non-multipart requests and queried a phantom `users` collection via the public client — now content-type-guarded (clean 400 "Request must be multipart/form-data…"), `users`→`members`, `consuela_saved_locations` read wrapped in a `[]` fallback, all on `withAdmin`. `email/forward` got the same `users`→`members` + `withAdmin` fix (now 200 end-to-end). **(4) Removed dead/dangerous routes:** legacy `/api/family` route + `family.json` (zero callers; leaked plaintext PINs on GET and allowed unauthenticated arbitrary file writes on POST) and the dead `/api/consuela/chat` stub + its integration-test block (unauthenticated PB, phantom `conversations`/`conversation_messages`, hardcoded TODO reply — real chat uses `/api/hermes/chat`). **(5) chat/process hardened:** `/api/chat/process` (enhanced-AI clarification layer `UnifiedInput` calls before `/api/hermes/chat`) 500'd on phantom `consuela_family_members`/`consuela_saved_locations`; those reads now fall back to `[]` and the ambiguity check is skipped when no family context loads, so it passes through cleanly (200, no clarification spam) and chat still flows to Hermes. **Known limitations (graceful degradation, not crashes):** money-mountain + time-capsule collections were never seeded and their schemas use `relation` fields to a nonexistent `users` collection — both degrade to empty 200s (data doesn't persist); the enhanced-AI family (`family-ai`/`consuela-ai-enhanced` context enrichment) is a parallel demo built on phantom collections and only its crash paths were fixed. Verified live (adult session): all GET routes 200 or deliberate 404 on removed routes; gateway reads 200; skill-tree/schedule-analytics/recurring-patterns/family-memory return real data; family-memory create/read/delete round-trips; removed routes 404; formData routes clean 400. Suite 555/555 (removed 2 consuela/chat tests, updated pb-rules-lockdown mock for autodate), `tsc` clean, eslint clean on all touched files.

- 2026-08-26 — fix(integrations): Settings → Integrations backend repaired — live PB reseeded, seed script rewired to the canonical seeder, graceful degradation on config-store/HA failures. **Root cause:** the live PocketBase (recreated 2026-08-21) predated HA Phase 2 + Services & Keys and was missing 14 collections (`consuela_service_config`, `ha_notify_config`, `ha_notify_prefs`, `ha_mirror_state`, `ha_entities/areas/devices/automations`, `skill_tree_profiles`, `skill_branches`, `quests`, `achievements`, `user_achievements`, `recurring_patterns`), so every `/api/services/*` route threw → bare 500s (🔑 card dead, Test buttons dead, runtime 500 → weather hardcoded coords) and the 🔔 card warned "config store unreachable". Compounding: `npm run pb:seed` ran a stale standalone `scripts/pb-seed.mjs` (original 10 collections only, no LOCKED_RULES) while the complete `seedCollections()` in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` was wired to nothing. **(1)** `scripts/pb-seed.mjs` rewritten as a thin wrapper: auto-loads `.env.local` via `process.loadEnvFile` when NEXT_PUBLIC_PB_URL/PB_ADMIN_EMAIL/PB_ADMIN_PASS aren't exported (explicit env still wins for the `scripts/consuela/test-*.mjs` flow), validates, then dynamic-imports `seedCollections()` AFTER env resolution (pb.ts/pb-auth.ts capture env at module load). **(2)** Seed run against the live instance: 14 collections created; `events` patched +importanceScore/importanceReason/importanceUpdatedAt; `recipes` +sourceUrl; drifted rules re-locked admin-only on members/meal_plan_entries/events/tasks/emergency_contacts/schedules/rewards/penalties. **(3)** Hardening: `GET /api/services/config` try/catch → 503 `{services:[], error:"config_store_unreachable"}` (was empty-body 500); `ServicesKeysCard` gained a `loadFailed` one-line error state; `GET /api/services/runtime` try/catch → 200 `{runtime:{}, error:...}` (widgets keep defaults); `GET /api/ha/health` try/catch → 200 `{ok:false, configured:false, reason:"not_configured", wsStatus:"disconnected", wsConnected:false}` (was 500 when HA_HOST/HA_TOKEN absent). Verified live (adult session): config GET 200 all 11 services, PUT/DELETE round-trip with source=db, Test buttons honest (themealdb ok 434ms; hermes unreachable; telegram bot ok but chat-id unset; gmail/HA not_configured), notify-targets warning gone, notify-test honest 400, Google state/device-grant untouched. Tests +3 (config 503 path, runtime empty path, ha-health not_configured). Suite 557/557, tsc clean, 0 new lint (1 pre-existing set-state-in-effect in ServicesKeysCard legacy-blob effect, present on HEAD).

- 2026-08-24 — feat(settings): Services & Keys (plan docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-24-services-keys-settings.md, branch feat/services-keys-settings). consuela_service_config collection + registry whitelist (11 services, boot-critical six excluded); shared secret-box crypto; resolver (PB→env→null) migrated into ha/config+mqtt+rest-client+ws singleton getters (now async), free-communication, telegram/get-updates, hermes/chat (hardcoded key default deleted), instacart (localStorage split-brain removed), themealdb, weather coords (runtime endpoint + hook, 3 literals deduped). Routes: /api/services/config GET(masks)/PUT/DELETE (adult gate), /test per-service health fns, /import (legacy blob), /runtime, /home-assistant/reconnect. Settings 🔑 card w/ status dots, masked fields, Test/Clear/Reconnect, import banner. Compose passthroughs repaired both repos; .env.example completed; dead OPENROUTER/GOOGLE_POLL removed. connections/store kept as deprecated widget shim (9 widgets still read it — proxy migration is follow-up). Suite 550/550, tsc clean.

## Change Log (this manual only)

- 2026-08-27 — chore(data): all demo/seed data purged — dashboard starts completely clean. Removed code-level seeders and PB fallbacks for: tasks, meals, grocery, pantry, recipes, rewards, penalties, schedules, emergency contacts, proactive suggestions, morning briefings. Live PB purged of 117 demo records via Node.js admin script. Preserved: 9 real family members, 67 Google Calendar events, 3 service-config keys. New: one-time localStorage data epoch reset (`consuela-data-epoch`, epoch 1) in `SyncInit.tsx` wipes stale per-device caches on first load. Every screen starts empty with the existing empty-state UI. `DEPLOY_NAS_LOCAL.md` updated for the clean state. Branch: `warm-glass-v2` (commit `e47b708`).

- 2026-08-24 — feat(auth): session auth layer + PocketBase lockdown (10-task plan docs/superpowers/plans/2026-08-24-session-auth-pb-lockdown.md + addendum 9.5, branch feat/session-auth-pb-lockdown). New: src/lib/session.ts (edge-safe HMAC), /api/auth/{login,logout,whoami}, middleware /api/** gate, /api/db/[collection] gateway (sessioned, allowlisted, sanitized), /api/members/verify + /api/members/admin (adults-gated CRUD, last-parent guard), admin-auth accepts adult sessions, useAuth server login, emergency/tasks PIN checks server-side, PB LOCKED_RULES enforced by seed. Client bundle contains zero PINs; zero client-direct PB paths (audited). Suite 492/492, tsc clean, eslint clean on touched files.

- 2026-08-24 — feat(security): server-side login; PINs leave the client bundle (session-auth Task 9). `src/hooks/useAuth.tsx` — `login()` is now async and POSTs to `/api/auth/login` (PB-verified, httpOnly cookie); `AuthUser` drops `pin`; localStorage `consuela-auth-user` stores id/name/role/emoji/color/avatarSize/glow only; hydrate path sanitizes legacy stored objects; PB-restore path no longer fabricates a pin; `changePin()` removed (ProfileSheet posts current+new PIN to `/api/members/pin` itself). `PinModal` awaits the async login. `src/components/ui/EmergencyButton.tsx` — in-modal family-PIN input replaces the removed `currentUser.pin` (typed per alert; type buttons disabled until 4 digits). `useSuggestions.ts` — pin cache no longer seeded from auth context (needsPin modal flow unchanged). `src/app/api/emergency/route.ts` — verification swapped from the `db.selectMembers()` client-cache loop to `verifyPinAgainstAnyMember(pin)` (server/PB truth); invalid → **401** `Invalid PIN` (was 403); `EMERGENCY_PIN_BYPASS` behavior unchanged (env now read per request so tests can stub it). Client-bundle credential purge: `member-fallback.ts` + `db/fallback-data.ts` rows stripped of PINs; member cache seeding (`resolveMemberPin` injection) removed from `db/index.ts`/`pb-db.ts` fallback branches; seed-side defaults moved to `MEMBER_DEFAULT_PINS` in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` (server-only) applied by `server-auth.ts::withResolvedPins` so fresh installs still verify + `findOrCreateMemberRecord` still seeds real values into PB. Settings member edit: blank PIN field = "leave unchanged" (no wipe). Tests: new emergency-pin-server (4) + use-auth-login (2); emergency-notify re-pointed at the server verifier; scripts/consuela/test-member-pins.mjs rewritten for the no-default-client-pin contract. Suite 471/471, tsc clean. Known follow-up: Tasks-page completion/undo/claim/parent-approval still verify against PB-served cached pins client-side — needs its own server route.

- 2026-08-24 — fix(security,tier-2): admin-route PIN gate, honest Telegram delivery, Google 410 self-heal, server-authoritative task claims. **(1) /api/admin/* gated** (`src/lib/admin-auth.ts`): every route (version/containers/update/restart) now requires either `x-admin-pin` verified against a NON-child family member (Settings UI sends the session PIN; kids get 403 `adult_only` even with a valid PIN) or `Authorization: Bearer $ADMIN_SECRET` (server-only env, used by chat-tool internal self-fetches in hermes-tools.ts). Fail-closed when neither is configured. `ADMIN_SECRET` added to docker-compose (required) + .env.example. Tests: admin-auth (4) + admin-routes (8). **(2) Telegram failures are no longer reported as delivered** (`src/lib/ha/notify.ts`, `notify-test/route.ts`): `sendTelegramMessage` returns `{success:false}` instead of throwing, but callers only counted thrown errors — a dead bot token showed "Sent ✓" including on the EMERGENCY path. broadcastHouseAlert now checks `.success` (failed leg lands in notes), notify-test returns 502 on failure. Tests: ha-notify + new ha-notify-test-route (2). **(3) Google sync survives expired tokens**: `googleFetch` errors now carry `.status`; `syncCalendar` catches HTTP 410 GONE, discards the stale syncToken and performs a full-window resync in the same run (previously every cron run failed forever until manual DB surgery). Non-410 errors still fail loudly. Tests: google-calendar-410 (2). **(4) Task claims are server-authoritative** (`api/tasks/claim`): points/title come from the PB task row (client body ignored — no more forged `points:999999`); unknown taskId → 404; post-write re-read detects a lost concurrent write race and reports an honest 409 `claimedBy` instead of silently erasing the first claimant's points. Tests: task-claim (4). Suite: 428/428, tsc clean, eslint clean.


- 2026-08-24 — fix(ops): tier-1 backend fixes — PB SDK crash, cron auth fail-open, compose conflict. **(1)** `autoCancellation()` removed everywhere (`src/lib/pb.ts`, `src/lib/pb-auth.ts` + 2 test mocks): the method no longer exists in pocketbase JS SDK v0.26+ (installed 0.27.0 — auto-cancellation was removed upstream and requests are never auto-cancelled by default, so deletion is behavior-identical). Root cause of the long-standing integration-test crash `cachedAdminPB.autoCancellation is not a function`; underneath it sat a second masked layer: vitest provided no PB admin credentials, so `ensureAuth()` threw after the first crash was cleared. Fixed by adding dummy `PB_ADMIN_EMAIL`/`PB_ADMIN_PASS` to `vitest.config.ts` `test.env` (tests mock `@/lib/pb` entirely — no network). **Test suite now fully green: 407/407.** **(2)** Cron bearer gate fail-closed: new shared `src/lib/cron-auth.ts` `isCronAuthorized()` rejects ALL traffic when `CRON_SECRET` is unset (previously the literal header `"Bearer undefined"` authenticated on 5 routes: ha-todo-mirror, briefing, google-sync, suggestions, telegram-poll); all six cron routes including score-importance now use it. Unit-tested in `tests/unit/cron-auth.test.ts`; score-importance route-level tests still pass unchanged. **(3)** `Home-ai/docker-compose.yml`: resolved stale HEAD-vs-kitchen-ui conflict markers (HEAD side had corrupted `${PB_ADM...D}` placeholders and wrong var name `PB_ADMIN_PASSWORD`) by taking the kitchen-ui side; `PB_ADMIN_EMAIL/PASS` and `CRON_SECRET` are now required (`${VAR:?…}` fails fast) instead of shipping `changeme` defaults; YAML validated with js-yaml.

- 2026-08-24 — fix(security,ha): alarm arm/disarm is human-only and PIN-gated. `alarm_control_panel` removed from the `/api/ha/call-service` allowlist (`src/lib/ha/service-allowlist.ts`) — the general unauthenticated route now 403s every alarm service. New `POST /api/ha/alarm` (`src/app/api/ha/alarm/route.ts`): validates action ∈ {arm_home→alarm_arm_home, disarm→alarm_disarm} + entity_id `^alarm_control_panel\.[a-z0-9_]+$`, verifies a family-member PIN server-side via `verifyPinAgainstAnyMember` (missing/wrong → 401 `unauthorized`), then forwards over the HA WebSocket. UI: shared `AlarmPinModal` (`src/components/ha/AlarmPinModal.tsx`) + `useAlarmCall()` hook (`useHAState.ts`); SecurityPanel and HomeSecurityWidget arm/disarm buttons now require PIN confirmation (kid mode unchanged — buttons hidden). LLM/chat tools never receive an alarm tool. Tests (TDD): new `tests/unit/ha-alarm-route.test.ts` (7 cases), ha-routes +2 blocked-alarm cases, allowlist tests updated, ha-tab/ha-widgets rewritten to the PIN-modal contract ("no fetch until PIN submitted"). Verified: 403/404 tests pass (1 pre-existing pb-auth failure), tsc clean, eslint 0 on touched files.

- 2026-08-24 — fix(security,ha): /api/ha/call-service now enforces a domain/service allowlist. New `src/lib/ha/service-allowlist.ts` (`HA_ROUTE_ALLOWED_SERVICES` + `isHAServiceAllowed`) and enforcement in `src/app/api/ha/call-service/route.ts` (403 `service_not_allowed` before the HA WebSocket forward). Allowed: light/switch (toggle/turn_on/turn_off), climate (set_temperature/set_hvac_mode), vacuum (start/pause/stop/return_to_base), alarm_control_panel (alarm_arm_home/alarm_disarm — Security panel). Blocked: lock.*, script.*, automation.trigger, shell_command.*, notify.*, cover.*, scene/media_player via this route, alarm_arm_away/alarm_trigger. Previously ANY `^[a-z0-9_]+$` domain/service was forwarded unauthenticated (the "alarms and locks excluded" guarantee existed only in the chat tool matrix). Internal trusted callers (todo-mirror, notify fan-out) use the WS client directly and are unaffected; chat house-control tools unchanged. Tests: new `tests/unit/ha-service-allowlist.test.ts` (5) + 7 route cases in `tests/unit/ha-routes.test.ts`; suite 394/395 (1 pre-existing pb-auth autoCancellation failure), typecheck + eslint clean on touched files.

- 2026-08-21 — fix(ui): Calendar member strip duplicate "All" chip removed. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — the member filter strip rendered the hardcoded "All" chip AND a second "All" entry that arrives in the members list (both `DEFAULT_CALENDAR_MEMBERS` and `db.selectMembersForCalendar()` prepend `{ name: "All" }`), so the strip showed "👥 All · 👨👩👧👦 All · Rebecca · …". Fix: the map skips entries named "All" (`members.filter((m) => m.name !== "All")`). Verified via Playwright at 390/1440: single All chip + 7 member chips; typecheck clean (the one eslint error at line 548 is pre-existing — the mount-effect Google auto-sync). Also audited the rest of the page (month grid 34 day cells, Today panel, Upcoming 7-day cards, Family Routines schedule tab) — no overlaps, no clipping, no overflow.

New House tab `/ha` ("Home Controls" — Overview/Security/Climate/Lights/Automation tabs; RoomCards + RoomSheet, SecurityPanel with prominent Arm home/Disarm, ClimateCard, LightsGrid) added to CapsuleNav between Calendar and Settings and hidden for the child role (kid mode keeps 6 tabs). - 2026-08-21 — fix(ops): Home rendering + nav color restored after Turbopack CSS desync. Symptom: entire app unstyled (bottom nav lost its neon-lime pill, widgets stacked in raw DOM order — "calendar at the top of Home", same breakage on every page). Root cause: the long-running `next dev` server (started 07:08) went out of sync with the committed CSS chunks after the HA feature's commit churn (the known Next 16.2.6/16.3 Turbopack CSS-manifest bug documented in the Build Note above) — served HTML linked only the framework chunk and dropped the globals.css chunk entirely (`--capsule-accent` computed empty). Fix: restarted `next dev` (kill PID 4257 → `npm run dev`). Verified via Playwright after restart: `--capsule-accent: #78f05a` + active pill `rgb(120,240,90)` + `nav.fixed` restored; Home, /calendar, /tasks, /meals, /chat, /settings all clean at 1440/768/390 (0 non-HA console errors, no horizontal overflow). **Not touched (other agent's in-flight HA feature):** `/api/ha/sync` still 502s because `HA_HOST`/`HA_TOKEN` are absent from `.env.local` (`getHAConfig()` throws) — the HA widgets render their graceful empty/error states; flagged to user, left for the HA workstream.

- 2026-08-21 — fix(db): PocketBase access rules repaired — Home no longer shows demo fallback data. `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` — new `PUBLIC_RULES` const (all five rules = `""`) + `rulesMatch(live)` self-heal in `seedCollections()`: newly created collections get open rules, and existing collections whose rules aren't open are patched (`(rules opened)` marker in the seed log). Root cause: the PB instance was recreated 2026-08-21 (collections created 11:03–11:32 with default closed `null` rules via `pb_migrations`), so every unauthenticated client read (`getPB()` — the app has no client PB auth) returned 403, `safeList` fell back to hardcoded demo data, and the Home screen showed fake events ("Soccer Practice", "Dentist — Bailey", "Team dinner"), fake tasks ("Take out trash", "Grocery run"), and no "Upcoming important" section (its public-client `getList` 403'd into the empty catch). Re-ran `seedCollections()` against the live instance: rules opened on all 22 app collections, missing `auth_sessions` collection created, grocery/pantry/meal/task schema fields patched. Verified via Playwright: 0 PB 4xx/5xx, Today widget shows "Quiet day" + the Upcoming important section (Doctor appointment / Game vs Riverside / Parent Teacher Conference with dates and members), fallback demo markers gone, no horizontal overflow at 1440/768/390; `tsc --noEmit` clean, eslint clean. **Security note (flagged to user):** open rules restore the pre-existing design (client has no auth; data incl. member PIN fields is readable on the LAN). Hardening (auth layer + auth-based rules) is future work.

- 2026-08-20 — fix(ui): weather widget fits its 1×1 bento cell (sun on card, no overflow). Root cause: `WeatherWidget` rendered the centered icon strip OUTSIDE the immersive card (in-flow, ~116px) while the card itself was `h-full` (350px), so icon + card = ~466px in a 350px `auto-rows-[350px]` grid cell — the sun floated in empty space and the card spilled ~116px onto the widget below (tablet + desktop; phone `auto-rows-min` was unaffected). Fix (`src/components/ui/WeatherWidget.tsx`): moved the icon strip INSIDE the card (first in-flow child above the glassmorphism overlay, `z-30`, `pt-3`, icon box 96→80px / scale 1.3333→1.1111) so the sun sits on the card and the whole widget is a single `h-full` flex column that fits exactly 350px. Verified: card top/bottom align with its grid cell (358/708 @1440, no overflow) at 1440/768/390; weather unit tests 2/2; vitest 167/168 (1 pre-existing PB-env); typecheck clean.

- 2026-08-18 — fix(ui): Kitchen "orb" true root cause — glass ::before position containment. `src/app/globals.css` — added `position: relative` to `.material-thin/.glass-subtle`, `.material-regular/.glass`, `.material-thick/.glass-strong`. Root cause: those base classes are `position: static`, so their `::before` specular (`position:absolute; inset:0; radial-gradient(120% 60% at 20% 0%); mix-blend-mode:screen`) resolved `inset:0` against the nearest positioned ancestor `<main class="relative z-10">` (full page), painting a giant top-left glow on every `.glass` card (Meals stat tiles + meal cards, Pantry stat/item cards). Grocery's top cards are `.widget-card` (no `::before`) → always clean. Containment removes the page-wide orb; also calmed `.glass::before` 0.05→0.02, `.liquid-glass::before` 0.06→0.02, `.widget-card` glow 40px@22%→20px@12% (dark) / hover 48px@28%→24px@14% / light 32px@16%→16px@8%. Verified via Playwright: Meals + Pantry top-left now flat dark, matching Grocery. AGENTS.md snapshot + UI Change Record updated.

Verified: typecheck clean, lint 0 new (73 baseline), vitest 149/150 (1 pre-existing PB-env integration failure), build clean, Playwright smoke (Home hides toggled-off widget; knob animates). AGENTS.md snapshot + UI Change Record updated.

- 2026-08-18 — fix(ui): Settings Layout & display — reorder arrows work with real clicks (drag scoped to the ⋮⋮ handle). Root cause: the Visible-group row `<div>` in `src/app/settings/page.tsx` was `draggable` with `onDragStart` — any mousedown + a few px of movement on a child control (↑/↓ arrows, visibility toggles) fired `dragstart` on the row and cancelled the child's click (Playwright event log: `mousedown: BUTTON → dragstart: DIV`, no `click` ever fired — real mouse/trackpad clicks almost always jitter 2–5px, so the arrows only worked for pixel-perfect clicks). Fix: `draggable` + `onDragStart` moved from the row `<div>` to the ⋮⋮ handle `<span>`; the row keeps `onDragOver` / `onDragLeave` / `onDrop` / `onDragEnd` (`dragend` bubbles from the handle, so drop targeting + cleanup unchanged). Verified via Playwright: plain + jittered arrow clicks both reorder; handle drag-and-drop still reorders; 112/112 unit tests pass; typecheck clean; no new lint errors. AGENTS.md snapshot + UI Change Record updated.

- 2026-08-11 — feat(ui): Three-mode Home layouts — phone / tablet / desktop buckets (5 commits: `test(ui)` layout-config 3-mode rewrite (red, 23 tests — replaces the 4 stale failures from the 2026-08-07 filmstrip change) → `feat(ui)` 3-mode core → `feat(ui)` v3 storage → `refactor(ui)` useOrientation/computeLayoutMode + LayoutMode rename → `feat(ui)` settings Tablet bucket). `src/lib/layout-config.ts` — `LayoutMode = "phone" | "tablet" | "desktop"`; `PHONE_MAX_WIDTH = 700` / `TABLET_MAX_WIDTH = 1280`; pure `computeLayoutMode(isPortrait, width)` (landscape OR ≥1280 → desktop; portrait <700 → phone; portrait 700–1279 → tablet; SSR via the hook assumes desktop). `HomeLayoutConfig` = `{ phone: { widgets }, tablet: { widgets }, desktop: { widgets } }` stored as v3 in `consuela-home-layout` (plain JSON via `saveLayoutConfig` — no version constant). Migration in `loadLayoutConfig`: v1 `{ widgets }` → all three buckets; v2 `{ portrait, landscape }` → phone=portrait, desktop=landscape, tablet = `toTabletOrder`-partitioned portrait (hole-free, full-width-first); v3 app-written configs round-trip exactly (id validation only, no self-heal reorder). Tablet render = 2-col bento (`grid grid-cols-2`) with morningBriefing / weather / consuelaSuggestions full-row (`TABLET_COL_SPANS` `col-span-2`) + full-width This Week footer; phone = `grid-cols-1` stack; desktop = horizontal filmstrip (unchanged); pre-mount `HOME_GRID_FALLBACK` = `grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3`. `src/hooks/useOrientation.tsx` — returns `{ orientation: LayoutMode; mounted }`; useHomeLayout gains `widgetsFor(mode)` + mode-aware mutators; the `Orientation` type name is fully removed. `src/app/settings/page.tsx` — "Layout & display" gains 📱 Phone / 📱 Tablet / 🖥️ Desktop tabs, reset toast "Layout reset for phone, tablet, and desktop", export JSON includes all three modes. Verified: 138/138 unit tests, typecheck clean, browser checks 26/26. AGENTS.md snapshot + UI Change Record updated.

- 2026-08-07 — feat(ui): Full-width desktop shell + uniform bento rhythm. `PageShell.tsx` — desktop cap widened `md:max-w-3xl`→`md:max-w-3xl lg:max-w-none`; the landscape bento now fills the whole viewport on desktop (grid right edge lands at the same 16px-from-screen-edge as the fixed `top-4 right-4` Emergency button; verified via Playwright at 1440px: both edges at 16px, grid width 1408px). `page.tsx` — removed portrait-only top margins on four widgets (`MorningBriefingSlot` `mt-3 lg:mt-0`; leaderboard + consuelaSuggestions cases `mt-3 lg:mt-0`; Today's Events `SectionCard className="mt-4 lg:mt-0"`); those added 12–16px on top of the grid's `gap-6` 24px row-gap in portrait, producing 36/40px gaps while landscape was already uniform at 24px via `lg:mt-0`. - 2026-08-05 — fix(consuela): final sweep regressions — PIN flow for suggestions, engine condition-dedup, minors (consuela-integration branch, one commit). **C3 client PIN flow:** `useSuggestions.ts` now keeps an in-memory PIN cache (seeded from `currentUser.pin`, never persisted), exposes `needsPin` + `submitPin(pin)`; when no PIN is available the pending dismiss/snooze/act is queued and a new shared `SuggestionPinModal` (mirrors the tasks-page PIN modal; guest copy "Enter any family member's 4-digit PIN") is rendered by the Home widget and /suggestions page; a 401 clears the cached pin and re-prompts with "Wrong PIN. Try again." (never a silent no-op); non-401 failures surface a toast. **C1 engine condition-dedup:** `runEngine` now fetches all pending rows (`status="pending"`, any scopeDate/snooze state) and skips inserts whose `kind` + normalized title (trim + lowercase, mirroring the hash normalization) already have a pending row — exactly one pending row per condition, so persistent conditions no longer accumulate one visible row per day; snoozing hides the single row; acting/dismissing frees the condition for re-creation. **Minors:** M-A `complete_task` "already completed" copy is now generic ("earlier this week or previously") since `done` rows include same-week completions; M-B the suggestions cron route + engine now use `localTodayISO()` (matching the briefing generator) so 5pm–midnight local scans land on today's local scopeDate; M-C `/api/consuela/briefing` validates `scopeDate` against `^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$` (400 `{error:"invalid scopeDate"}`, mirrors the L10 threadId hardening); M-D the suggestions PATCH wraps `db.updateSuggestion` in try/catch → 400 `{ok:false,error}` instead of a 500 (act route already did); M-E `public/version.json` regenerated. TS / lint / build clean; all 13 `scripts/consuela/` tests pass (test-engine.mjs +3 C1 dedup steps, test-suggestions.mjs +2 C3 client-flow + briefing-validation steps, test-chat-tool-call.mjs asserts the new M-A string) plus a Playwright DOM check of the PIN modal on both /suggestions and the Home widget (guest prompt, wrong-pin re-prompt, valid-pin retry, cancel).
- 2026-08-05 — fix(consuela): post-scan bug sweep — 17 fixes (consuela-integration branch, one commit). **Critical:** C1 `selectPendingSuggestions` dropped the `scopeDate>="today"` clause (snoozed rows survived past midnight; scopeDate is now an optional exact-match filter); C2 `/act` route + `action_suggestion` no longer pass `scopeDate: today` so past-day suggestions are findable by id; C3 `/api/consuela/suggestions` PATCH + POST /act now require a family-member PIN (`x-consuela-pin` header or cookie, verified against PB; GET stays public) and the widget/page forward the session PIN. **Important:** I1 chat SYSTEM_PROMPT gained the "use a read tool to check current state" rule and `persistChatPair` skips empty/fallback replies; I2 Telegram poller got a 30s AbortController timeout, `r.ok` check, and 429 handling (offset untouched, `{ok:false, reason:"rate_limited"}`); I3 `insertChatMessage` accepts an optional `createdAt` (poll passes the real send time); I4 `complete_task` excludes `status:"done"` rows and returns "Task was already completed (last week)…" instead of double-earning; I5 `action_suggestion` treats `ok:false` as failure and leaves the suggestion pending; I6 `setState` gained an expected-prev CAS arg (telegram offset writes refuse concurrent advances); I7 new `src/lib/local-date.ts` (`localTodayISO` via `en-CA`/TZ) anchors suggestions + briefings + widget refresh to the local calendar day. **Minor:** L1 dedup hash now includes severity + action args; L2 conflict scanner uses `<30` boundary + overnight spill detection (≥23:00 prev day vs <01:30 today); L3 briefing cron is idempotent (existing row → skip, ack preserved); L4 briefing ack is optimistic with rollback + "Couldn't save — try again" toast; L5 briefing empty sections render nothing (no blank gaps); L6 layout self-heal inserts `morningBriefing` at index 0 / `consuelaSuggestions` at index 1 (not appended); L7 Google quota counter computes DATE per call + serialized queue (no lost updates); L8 google-sync gained an in-process lock (concurrent cron fire → `{ok:false, reason:"already_in_progress"}`); L9 chat page dead `actions`/`runAction` code removed; L10 `/api/chat/messages` rejects malformed `threadId` (400) + Telegram error messages redact the bot token. AGENTS.md §3.5 (UTC thread ids), §5.2 (PIN requirement), §5.3 (tool-call transcripts not persisted) updated. TS / lint / build clean; all 13 `scripts/consuela/` tests pass with new per-finding assertions.
- 2026-08-05 — fix(consuela): final review sweep (consuela-integration branch). **F1 snooze:** `useSuggestions.ts` snooze now sends only `snoozedUntil` (row stays `status:"pending"`; the `(snoozedUntil=null || snoozedUntil<now)` filter hides it until it expires); `/api/consuela/suggestions` PATCH accepts id + snoozedUntil without status. **F2 conflict parser:** `engine.ts` `parseMinutes` mirrors `parseTimeToMinutes` (12-hour AM/PM aware — 12:45 PM vs 1:15 PM now detected); regression events added to test-engine.mjs. **F3 complete_task:** hermes-tools `complete_task` now updates the `tasks` row (`status:"done"`, `completedInWeek`, `completedAt`) after the week_data earn so tasks leave Pending and can't double-earn; 2nd `tasks` seed schema gained `status` (select pending/done) + `completedInWeek` + `completedAt`; test-chat-tool-call.mjs asserts the row flip + single earn tx. **F4 act button:** new `POST /api/consuela/suggestions/act` (dispatches `actionPayload.tool` via `getTool`, marks the suggestion `actioned`, 400 on error with the suggestion left pending); Home widget + /suggestions page act buttons now POST + toast + refresh; navigation kept only for view tools (get_pending_tasks / get_weekly_meals / open_calendar). **Minors:** GET /api/consuela/suggestions honors `?limit=` (1..200, default 20); `action_suggestion` only marks `actioned` when the tool result has no error (tool throw/error leaves it pending); briefing GET defaults to today's scopeDate (null when today's briefing doesn't exist); `public/version.json` regenerated to branch HEAD; `docker-compose.yml` wires `TELEGRAM_MIRROR_BOT_TOKEN` (empty default); `next.config.ts` gains `allowedDevOrigins: ["127.0.0.1","localhost"]` (fixes dev-server client-bundle blocking that broke Playwright UI tests hitting 127.0.0.1); test-chat-route-mock.mjs counts only Hermes fetches (route persists chat pairs to PB, skewing the old global fetch count). TS / lint / build clean; all 13 `scripts/consuela/` tests pass.

Applied to global chrome (`BottomNav`, `ConsuelaFAB`, `EmergencyButton`, `Stepper`, `SegmentedControl`), core UI kit (`SoftButton`, `IconButton`, `Chip`, `ListRow`, `Surface`, `ThemeToggle`, `TopBar`), and Kitchen controls (`MealsTab`, `GroceryTab`, `PantryTab`, `RecipesTab` filter chips, sync buttons, copy/duplicate/day-picker buttons, list checkboxes, action icons). Replaces the previously mixed `active:scale-95/90`, `hover:scale-105`, `hover:brightness-110`, and missing focus rings. Deliberate exclusions: `Avatar` + `Badge` (decorative non-focusable), `Toggle` (already correct), `Toast`/`TextField` (non-interactive / own ring). `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-17-stream-c-interaction-consistency-design.md` written. AGENTS.md "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + new UI Change Record updated. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run lint` clean, `npm run build` clean.

- 2026-06-15 — fix(ui): Home greeting + family strip respond to login state. Greeting left-of-title emoji used to be a hard-coded 👋; now reads `dashboardCurrentUser.emoji` when `isLoggedIn` (e.g. 🐱 for Rebecca, 👩 for an adult, etc.) and falls back to 👋 when signed out. Family avatar strip below the greeting used to always show all 6 members + `＋` chip; now filters to a single avatar matching the signed-in member (using the existing `memberMatchesName` helper) and hides the `＋` chip when `isLoggedIn`. - 2026-06-15 — fix(grocery): adding an item no longer wipes the list. `src/hooks/useGrocery.ts` `addGroceryItem` was replacing the entire UI state with the PocketBase cache via `setGroceryItems(fresh.length ? fresh : [...groceryItems, ...])`; because the 11 default items in `initialGroceryItems` are local-only and the `grocery_list_items` PB collection has no schema fields (PB silently drops `name`/`category`/`emoji` on create and returns only `{id, collectionId, collectionName}`), the new item landed as a nameless 📦 and the rest of the list vanished. Rewrote `addGroceryItem` to use functional `setGroceryItems(prev => [...prev, newItem])` and build the new item from the local input. Added `mergeFreshIntoState` and routed `syncMealToGrocery` / `syncPantryToGrocery` through it so sync paths merge DB items by name instead of replacing local-only items. Extended `seedCollections` in `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` to diff `live.schema` against the seed schema and `pb.collections.update({ schema: merged })` to add only the missing fields — re-running the seed is now self-healing. AGENTS.md "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + new UI Change Record updated. TS / lint clean.
- 2026-06-15 — fix(ui): Home widget spacing (Quick ask → Today gap). Default Home layout puts Quick ask (Consuela ask) above Leaderboard above Today's Events (📅 calendar card); `space-y-6` only gave 24px between each, so the three cards felt cramped. Wrapped the Leaderboard case in `<div className="mt-3">` (36px above Leaderboard) and gave the Today SectionCard `className="mt-4"` (40px above Today). Margins travel with each widget so the spacing holds in any user-reordered layout. Verified at 375px via Playwright. AGENTS.md "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + new UI Change Record updated. TS / lint clean.
- 2026-06-15 — fix(ui): Weather widget atmospheric bridge now follows the widget when reordered. `<AtmosphericBridge />` used to be rendered once at the top of the widget stack (`{widgets.length > 0 && <AtmosphericBridge />}` before the `widgets.map(...)` in `src/app/page.tsx`), so when the user moved Weather away from position 1 the 18px gradient + drifting-particles strip stayed pinned to whatever widget was now first — visually disconnecting the spillover from the weather card it was supposed to bleed out of. Fix: the bridge is now rendered inside the `case "weather"` switch case, directly below `<WeatherWidget />` inside the same `<div className="relative z-10">` wrapper. Bridge `marginTop: -2px / marginBottom: -4px` overlap still flows the gradient and seasonal `theme.particleEmoji` into whatever widget sits immediately below weather in user order. Move weather to position 3 → bridge follows to position 3. The fullscreen `FogBackground` Vanta fog (page-wide ambient, `position: fixed; inset: 0`) is intentionally untouched per user request. AGENTS.md "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + new UI Change Record updated. TS / lint clean (only pre-existing warnings remain).
- 2026-06-15 — fix(ui): Consuela chat input box now sits above the bottom nav. `src/app/chat/page.tsx` input wrapper `paddingBottom` raised from `calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 4.5rem)` (~72px) to `calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 7rem)` (128px) so the input pill clears the 6-tab BottomNav (which is `mb-3 + py-4 + h-14` ≈ 100px above the safe area). Added `z-50` to the wrapper so its glass surface paints above the `BottomNav` (`fixed bottom-0 z-50`). The input pill, mic, and send button are now always visible and tappable on the Ask Consuela chat screen. AGENTS.md "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + new UI Change Record updated. TS / lint clean.
- 2026-06-15 — fix(ui): Kitchen tabs mobile overflow. Grocery / Meals / Pantry tab grids all used `grid gap-5/6 xl:grid-cols-[…] / lg:grid-cols-[…]` without a `grid-cols-1` base; on a 375px viewport the implicit grid columns were sized by form content (native `<select>` min-widths blocked the `flex-1` parents from shrinking), pushing each child to ~552–786px and clipping the right-rail widgets. Fixed by adding `grid-cols-1` to the outer grid + `min-w-0` to both grid children in `GroceryTab.tsx`, `MealsTab.tsx`, and `PantryTab.tsx`, plus `shrink-0` on the form `<select>` / "Add" button rows and `min-w-0` on the flex-1 `<input>` fields. Result: "Sync from Meals" / "Sync from Pantry" / "Mark all as needed" all read fully on 375px, "0 of 2 picked up" subtext no longer clipped, no page-level horizontal scroll. AGENTS.md "Current Dashboard Snapshot" + new UI Change Record updated. TS / lint clean.
- 2026-06-15 — fix(ui): schedule time picker. Calendar → Schedule "Add / Edit" form now uses a 3-field 12-hour picker (Hour 1–12, Minute 00–59, AM/PM toggle) instead of a free-text military-time input. New items are stored in `"H:MM AM/PM"` form; the empty default changed from `"08:00"` to `"8:00 AM"`. Home widget `ScheduleDisplay` runs every schedule time through a `displayTime12h` helper, so freshly created 12-hour items and any pre-existing 24-hour data render consistently as 12-hour. `parseTimeToMinutes` in both files now accepts both `"H:MM AM/PM"` and `"HH:MM"`, so sort / category bucketing / past-item filtering keep working regardless of stored format. Time cell on the widget grew from `w-12` to `w-16` to fit "12:00 PM" cleanly. New `.calendar-time-picker`, `.calendar-time-hour`, `.calendar-time-minute`, `.calendar-time-sep`, `.calendar-time-ampm`, `.calendar-time-ampm-btn` rules in `globals.css` (active AM/PM uses `--color-accent-button` and a soft accent glow). - 2026-06-15 — feat(infra): PocketBase migration scaffolding landed. Replaced the openclaw proxy with a real PocketBase container on :8090 with a `pb_data` volume, dropped `NEXT_PUBLIC_OPENCLAW_BRIDGE_URL` from the Dockerfile, added a standalone `Dockerfile.deploy`, and introduced `src/lib/pb.ts` (singleton client), `src/lib/pb-auth.ts` (cached superuser auth, 1h TTL), `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` + `scripts/pb-seed.mjs` (idempotent collection bootstrap for members / meal_plan_entries / recipes / grocery_items / pantry_items / schedule_items / task_transactions), and `src/db/pb-db.ts` (safe read-through with in-memory fallback). The in-memory db is untouched; consumers continue to read from `src/db/index.ts` until individual modules are ported.
- 2026-06-15 — feat(tasks): leaderboard world-building landed as two commits — a toolkit commit (types, utils, podium/your-card/row/member-sheet/level-up-modal/share-card/daily-quest/streak-saver/catch-up-nudge/treasure-path/family-goal/achievement-wall/hall-of-fame/trophy-case + `useLeaderboardData` hook) and a Tasks page rewrite that wires the toolkit into Pending / Completed / Leaderboard / Rewards / Penalties tabs with confetti completion animation, parental approval gate, animated champion crown, and Home sync. The auth context now exposes `sessionRemainingMs`, `sessionWarning`, and `extendSession()` so the Home header can show a tappable inactivity countdown. `SwipeableRow` dedupes pointer/touch events so Cancel no longer reopens the PIN modal. `SectionCard` gained an `action` prop. `ProgressRing` clamp/format polished.
- 2026-06-15 — feat(meals): planner reliability + motion removal. `RecipeModal` targets meal vs catalog via a `mode` prop; `MealsTab` adds a collapsed `RecipeCatalogStrip` with one-tap slot insertion; `usePantry` writes to the DB and exposes a 'Grocery items waiting to be restocked' panel (delete is two-tap, no modal); `useGrocery` accepts `plannedMeals` so sync reflects the actual plan and includes units on quantities; manual add supports qty/notes + Auto category + duplicate handling; `useRecipes` normalizes and dedupes on save; `Meal` interface gains `mealType` / `protein` / `carbs` / `fat` / `instructions`. All planner tabs (Meals / Grocery / Pantry / Recipes) had float/bob/scale/translate animations removed.
- 2026-06-15 — feat(ui): layout, chat, and design-system polish. Wrapped the app in `LayoutProvider` so the Settings Layout & display card can read the home widget config anywhere. Theme init script is now a plain inline `<script>` (the previous `next/script strategy="beforeInteractive"` was a no-op in the app router). Added kid-friendly CSS animations for the leaderboard world: confetti-fall, crown-glow, badge-sparkle, rank-pulse, progress-fill, level-up-pop, podium-shine, rank-arrow-bounce, widget-row-glow, number-roll, session-pill-warning — all with prefers-reduced-motion overrides. `_design-system` DayStrip demo no longer drops its click handler.
- 2026-06-15 — fix: Layout & display reorder broken. Settings → Layout & display now reflects the user's saved order (was iterating the static `ALL_WIDGETS` master list), the ↑/↓ disabled state is computed from the user's position (was using the static index), and hidden widgets have their reorder controls disabled. Added Visible / Hidden grouping, a ⋮⋮ native drag-and-drop handle, toast feedback on every action, debounced (250ms) localStorage persistence with `pagehide` flush, suppressed rehydration while on `/settings`, and `loadLayoutConfig` validation that drops unknown ids and self-heals missing defaults. Weather is no longer pinned to the top of the Home page — it now respects the user's chosen position via a regular switch case. The duplicate "Reset layout" button was removed from Data & sync. `src/lib/layout-config.ts` gained `moveWidgetTo`, `getVisibleWidgets`, `getHiddenWidgets`. `src/hooks/useHomeLayout.tsx` gained `reorder`, `visibleWidgets`, `hiddenWidgets`, `setSuppressRehydrate`. Help modal copy updated to match the new contract.
- 2026-06-15 — feat: Home logout button + inactivity countdown surfaced + spacing rhythm. Replaced the hidden `onDoubleClick={logout}` avatar gesture with a visible `IconButton` (door + arrow, glass, sm) in the top-right Home header that opens a confirm `Modal` ("Sign out of {name}?"). Tapping the avatar itself now opens the PIN flow to switch profile. `useAuth` now exposes `sessionRemainingMs`, `sessionWarning`, and `extendSession()`; the Home header shows a small `⏳ mm:ss` pill after 60s of idle, amber-pulsing in the last 30s with a tappable "tap to stay" `Toast`. Home spacing standardized: stat-tile row `gap-2→gap-3`, family strip `gap-2→gap-3`, section card stack `space-y-5→space-y-6`, action row `gap-2→gap-3`, header `pb-5→pb-6`, greeting `mt-5→mt-6`. New `.session-pill-warning` keyframe in `globals.css` (reduced-motion safe). No new dependencies. *(Split across the leaderboard toolkit + Tasks rewrite commits above on 2026-06-15.)*
- 2026-06-15 — feat: Tasks & Leaderboard v2. Major refactor delivering week-scoped points, real consecutive-day streak tracking, transaction history log, undo completion with PIN, recurring task auto-regeneration at week start, parental approval gate for large rewards (>100pts), confetti completion animation, level-based progression with progress bars, achievement badges, animated champion crown, and Home page task sync. New files: `src/types/tasks.ts`, `src/lib/task-utils.ts`. Updated: `src/app/tasks/page.tsx`, `src/app/page.tsx`, `src/app/globals.css`. Fixed 23 logic issues (critical, contradictions, missing, edge cases). *(Now actually landed on `warm-glass-v2` as the two `feat(tasks):` commits above.)*
- 2026-06-15 — fix: Meal/recipe/grocery/pantry reliability + planner motion removal. *(Landed on `warm-glass-v2` as the `feat(meals):` commit above.)* `RecipeModal` now targets either meal or catalog via `mode` prop (was always saving as meal, corrupting meal state when editing catalog recipes). Recipes added from the modal go to the catalog only — assign to a slot later from Meals or Recipes tab. `MealsTab` gets a collapsed `RecipeCatalogStrip` above the day strip so users can browse the catalog while planning, with "Add to {activeDay} as {next empty slot}" one-tap insertion. `usePantry` now writes to the real DB (adds `db.deletePantryItem` to `src/db/index.ts` + `src/db/pb-db.ts`), merges DB + localStorage on load, and accepts `groceryItems` to power a "Grocery items waiting to be restocked" panel with one-tap "Add to pantry". Pantry delete is two-tap with a 3s confirmation window (no modal). `useGrocery` accepts `plannedMeals` from `useMeals` so `mealSyncService.syncMealPlanToGrocery` reflects what the user actually planned (was reading stale `db.selectMeals()`), and sync quantities now include units (`"1 cup"`, `"1 lb"`, etc.). Manual grocery add supports qty/notes inputs, "✨ Auto category" option, and friendly duplicate handling ("already on your list" toast + keep existing row). `useRecipes` normalizes tags/ingredients on save and dedupes by id then name. `GroceryTab`, `PantryTab`, `RecipesTab`, and `MealsTab` had all float/bob/scale/translate animations removed (color transitions, focus rings, and progress bars remain). `src/services/types.ts` `Meal` interface updated with `mealType`, `protein`, `carbs`, `fat`, `instructions`. `emptyRecipe` in `src/data/meals.ts` now includes `mealType`.

- 2026-06-15 — feat(integrations): Real Google Calendar + Tasks + Reminders sync. **Phase 1 of 3** from the API research plan. Replaces the static-JSON stub at `public/google-events.json` and `public/google-tasks.json` with real Google APIs. The previous `/api/google-calendar` route read those JSON files; the new version reads from a new `consuela_google_calendar_events` PB collection populated by `src/lib/google/calendar.ts:listAllEvents` + `syncCalendar`. The same architecture backs Tasks via `consuela_google_tasks`, `consuela_google_tasklists`, `consuela_google_sync_state`, and `consuela_google_api_usage` collections, all created idempotently on first request by `src/lib/google/pb-collections.ts:ensureGoogleCollections` (now uses `fields` + per-type `options` shape, since PB v0.27 silently drops schema fields missing the `options` key). **OAuth via Google Device Flow** (`src/lib/google/device-auth.ts`) so the home-hosted dashboard never needs a public redirect URI — the user signs in at `google.com/device` on any device with a one-time code. Required OAuth client type in Google Cloud Console: **"TVs and Limited Input Devices"** (Client ID + Client Secret). **Tokens are AES-256-GCM encrypted at rest** in `consuela_google_tokens` (`src/lib/google/encryption.ts`, key from `CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY` env var; 12 unit tests in `scripts/google/test-encryption.mjs`). `src/lib/google/oauth-client.ts` provides a singleton `googleFetch` wrapper that auto-refreshes the access token on 401 with a 30s in-flight lock to prevent thundering-herd refreshes. **5-minute client-side polling** via `useGoogleSync` hook + the `useGoogleReminders` hook on the Tasks page (auto-refreshes on tab visibility change too). New `Settings → Integrations` card (`GoogleConnectCard.tsx`) handles the full lifecycle: unconnected → waiting (code + countdown pill + copy/open buttons) → connected (account email + sync status + Sync now + Disconnect) → error:denied/expired/revoked/config. **Google Tasks is wired but paused** — Google's Device Flow allowed-scopes list does NOT include the Tasks API (confirmed via direct curl: `POST /device/code` with `https://www.googleapis.com/auth/tasks` returns `{"error":"invalid_scope","error_description":"Invalid device flow scope: https://www.googleapis.com/auth/tasks"}`). Calendar and openid/email work fine. Tasks code is complete (auto-creates a "Consuela" tasklist on first connect, Reminders = Tasks with `due` field, `<RemindersSection />` at the top of Pending groups by Overdue/Today/Tomorrow/This week/Later with one-tap complete). The Reminders section shows a "paused — Tasks scope not granted" notice with the upgrade path. To activate Tasks: switch the OAuth client to "Web" type in Google Cloud Console, expose the dashboard over a public HTTPS URL (Tailscale Funnel / Cloudflare Tunnel / ngrok paid tier), add the Tasks scope to `GOOGLE_OAUTH_SCOPES`, and reconnect. New API routes: `/api/google/device-grant`, `/api/google/device-poll`, `/api/google/device-revoke`, `/api/google/state`, `/api/google/sync`, `/api/google/webhook`, `/api/google-tasks`. Webhook route is pre-wired but not used in v1 (no public URL to receive Google's push); falls back to polling. Per-day API quota counter in `consuela_google_api_usage` (soft 50k, hard 100k) to prevent runaway loops from blowing the 1M/day Calendar free tier. Unit tests: 12/12 encryption + 8/8 device-auth passing. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run lint` clean, `npm run build` clean. New env vars in `.env.example`: `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID`, `GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET`, `GOOGLE_OAUTH_SCOPES` (Calendar-only by default), `CONSUELA_ENCRYPTION_KEY`, `GOOGLE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`. `tsconfig.json` got `allowImportingTsExtensions: true` so the new Google lib files can use `.ts` extensions on relative imports (lets the unit tests run via `node --experimental-strip-types`). `public/google-events.json` and `public/google-tasks.json` are no longer read by anything — deleted. `pb-auth.ts` got a `.ts` extension on its `pb` import for the same reason. **Live verification**: with the user's TVs OAuth client (project `graceful-mile-496715-k7`, client `255734025602-...us.googleusercontent.com`), the `/api/google/device-grant` endpoint returned a real Google `device_code` + `user_code: FFY-KPK-PXNS` (test run, code expired). User can now tap Settings → Integrations → Connect to get their own code. **Common Journey added**: "How do I connect Google Calendar + Tasks + Reminders?", "Why don't I see Reminders on the Tasks tab?", "How do I add a reminder?", "Why is the Reminders section on the Tasks tab showing 'paused'?"
- 2026-06-14 — fix: Leaderboard champion share ring. The “This week’s champion” card now calculates its ProgressRing from the visible leaderboard points, so the default leaderboard shows a meaningful champion share instead of 0%.
- 2026-06-14 — fix: Pending Tasks PIN modal reopens after Cancel. `SwipeableRow` now deduplicates pointer/touch finish events and resets swipe state on cancel/leave, so Cancel closes the modal without immediately reopening it.
- 2026-06-14 — fix: Day strip and swipe slide behavior. DayStrip now honors `value`/label matching, scroll snap, and `aria-pressed`; SwipeableRow now handles pointer/touch gestures, up/cancel/leave, and threshold callbacks for reliable slide actions.
- 2026-06-14 — fix: Tasks leaderboard penalty add/edit flows. Added working Add/Edit modals for rewards and penalties on the Tasks leaderboard, and kept penalty select labels free of raw avatar data URLs.
- 2026-06-14 — fix: Tasks avatar data URL leakage in filters. Filter chips now use safe emoji fallbacks instead of raw member avatar data URLs.
- 2026-06-14 — fix: Tasks page avatar data URL leakage. Member select options now display a safe emoji fallback instead of raw `data:image/...` avatar strings.
- 2026-06-14 — fix: Dark glass review card contrast. Raised the `/_design-system` dark glass sample from near-black to blue-charcoal, forced light text, and verified all review-page glass surfaces are readable.
- 2026-06-14 — fix: Design-system review route. Added public `/design-system` page plus `src/middleware.ts` rewrite so `/_design-system` works despite Next treating underscore folders as private. Cleaned review-page props, stable TextField IDs, and browser-console errors.
- 2026-06-14 — feat: Warm Glass v2 design system rollout. Added Warm Glass tokens, primitives, patterns, a dev-only `/_design-system` review page, simplified 5-tab bottom navigation plus More menu, and redesigned Home, Tasks, Meals, Settings, and More. Added `apricot` and `sage` accents. Verified with `npm run typecheck`, `npm run lint` (warnings only), and `npm run build`.
- 2026-06-13 — fix: Hydration mismatch on the Meals page (the "Who's eating tonight" avatar strip). Root cause: `src/db/index.ts:210-229` hydrates `membersStore` from `localStorage` at module load. On the server, `typeof window === "undefined"` falls back to `membersData` (defaults: 🐱, 🧒, etc.), but on the client it reads localStorage and gets the user's custom avatars (often base64 data URLs like `data:image/webp;base64,UklGR...`). `src/components/meals/MealsTab.tsx:92-95` was calling `db.selectMembersDetailed()` inside a `useMemo(..., [])`, so SSR rendered `🐱` and the client first render rendered the data URL — a React hydration mismatch (`+ data:image/webp...  − 🐱`). Fix: added the established `mounted` pattern (`useState(false)` + `useEffect(() => setMounted(true), [])`) and gated both the `familyMembers` and the `tip` `useMemo`s with it. Until mount, `familyMembers` returns the default emoji list (matches what the server saw) and `tip` returns `smartTips[0]` (deterministic, no `Date.now()`). After mount, both re-pick their real values. Other pages in the app already use this pattern (`tasks/page.tsx:216`, `page.tsx:43`, `chat/page.tsx`, `settings/page.tsx`, etc.) — MealsTab was the only consumer of `db.selectMembersDetailed()` that didn't. Heads up: `tasks/page.tsx:221` and `chat/page.tsx:289` also call `db.selectMembers()` in a `useMemo([], )`, but they are wrapped in an `if (!mounted) return <Loading/>` early return at the top of the component, so the read happens after mount and they are safe.
- 2026-06-13 — fix: Literal `\u2728` (and three sibling escapes) on the Calendar page. Four spots in `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` had unicode-escape strings written as **direct JSX text children** instead of inside a JavaScript expression, so React rendered the six literal characters `\u2728` instead of the ✨ emoji. JSX text is not interpreted as a JavaScript string — only `{`...`}` template literals (or other JS expressions) decode the escape. Fixed lines 507 (`calendar-member-avatar` 👥 in the "All" chip), 607 (`calendar-panel-icon` 📅 in the panel header), 618 (`calendar-empty-icon` ✨ in the "Nothing scheduled" empty state — the one the user reported), and 698 (the 🗑️ delete button label). All four now use the `{`\uXXXX`}` template-literal pattern that the rest of the file already uses (lines 542, 769-777, 798, 808, 838). No other `\uXXXX` JSX-text leaks exist in the codebase.
- 2026-06-13 — fix: Liquid Glass 2.2 — matched Consuela card shell. Tuned `.liquid-glass` in `src/app/globals.css` to share the soft glass shell of `.atmospheric-card` (the one used by the "Ask Consuela" card on the Home page), so the Today / Tasks / Daily Schedule rows (and every other `.liquid-glass` instance) feel like part of the same family while keeping their per-row color frost. Added the `0 0 30px rgba(255,255,255,0.03)` soft outer glow that `.atmospheric-card` has. The whisper of top sheen inside the card went from `0.18` → `0.06` in dark mode (matching `.atmospheric-card`) and from `0.45` → `0.10` in light mode — no longer a visible bright line. The `::before` specular layer was reduced to a breath of light at the top (`0.06` dark / `0.12` light) and trimmed from a 3-stop gradient to a 2-stop fade. Light-mode border softened from `0.30` → `0.20`. Hover state matches the new shell: outer glow `0.05`, top sheen `0.10` (dark), no bright white line. Per-row color frosting is unchanged — events still frost mint/violet/amber/cyan/rose/nori, tasks still frost rose/amber/mint, Daily Schedule rows still use their `item.color` — so the color identity of each row is preserved.
- 2026-06-13 — fix: Liquid Glass 2.1 — minimized white edge. Reduced the visible white frame around `.liquid-glass` cards in `src/app/globals.css` while keeping the glass depth. The 1px border went from `rgba(255,255,255,0.18)` → `0.06` in dark mode and `0.85` → `0.30` in light mode (no longer a hard white outline). The inner top sheen went from `0.55` → `0.18` in dark and `0.95` → `0.45` in light (a soft gradient, not a bright white line). The `::before` specular highlight was dialed back from `0.32` → `0.14` in dark and `0.55` → `0.28` in light. Removed the `inset 0 0 0 1px` inner ring layer entirely (was the source of a faint second white frame on the inside). Hover state matches: border `0.10` (dark), top sheen `0.28` (dark). The glass effect — backdrop blur `26px saturate(1.6)`, drop shadow lift, frosting tint — is unchanged.
- 2026-06-13 — feat: Liquid Glass 2.0. Strengthened `.liquid-glass` in `src/app/globals.css` so the Today / Tasks / Daily Schedule rows (and the chat quick-actions, recipe cards, pantry items, grocery sections that share the class) actually look like glass. Added a real external drop shadow (`0 8px 24px -8px rgba(0,0,0,0.45) + 0 2px 6px -2px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)`) that lifts the card off the background — the previous version was missing it. Bumped `backdrop-filter` from `blur(20px)` to `blur(26px) saturate(1.6)` so the frost tints read more richly. Added a glossy specular highlight via a `::before` radial gradient (white at 20% 0%, fading to 0 at 60% from the top, `mix-blend-mode: screen` in dark mode). Added a subtle bottom ambient via `::after` so the card reads as a chunk of glass, not a tinted rectangle. Strengthened the inner edge: brighter top inset highlight (0.55 in dark / 0.95 in light), a 1px inner ring (`inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(255,255,255,0.04)`) for the "lip of the glass" feel, and a stronger border. Hover now lifts 4px (was 3px) with a deeper drop shadow and brighter inner highlight. Bumped the color frosting on Today rows, Tasks rows, and Daily Schedule rows from `0.32 → 0.16` to `0.40 → 0.20` so the tint sits on the glass more confidently; bumped the Daily Schedule member pill from `0.45 → 0.25` to `0.55 → 0.30` so the pill reads against the stronger glass behind it. Reduced-motion still disables the lift, and the new pseudo-element layers are pointer-events:none so they never block clicks.
- 2026-06-12 — feat: Unified Appearance control (Display Mode + Time of Day merged). The standalone "Display Mode" radio list in Settings → Theme and the separate "Time of Day" segmented control in Settings → Weather are gone. Both are replaced by a single 3-option **Appearance** segmented control in the Weather card: `🌅 Auto` (system + auto, follows the real clock), `☀️ Day` (force light + day), `🌙 Night` (force dark + night). `useWeather.tsx` now resolves `timeOfDay === 'auto'` to the real local day/night before publishing the `__consuelaTod` hint, so the weather widget and the theme read the same clock (eliminates the previous drift where the theme fell back to its own seasonal sunrise/sunset table). `setAppearanceMode` writes both `theme.mode` and `weather.timeOfDay` together. Storage keys (`home-ai-theme-config`, `home-ai-weather-config`) are unchanged, so existing user data migrates automatically: `system + auto` → Auto, `light + day` → Day, `dark + night` → Night; any other combo is normalized to Auto on first render.

**End of Agent Operational Manual**

Remember: This file + the 2–3 linked deep docs it points to are sufficient for you to give perfect, up-to-date, safe instructions to any user of the Consuela dashboard. When in doubt, re-read this file first.

### Change Log (continued)

- 2026-06-17 — fix(ui): Schedule time picker hour resetting to 12. `src/app/calendar/page.tsx` — switched from controlled `value` + `onFocus/onBlur` to `defaultValue` + `key` (re-mounts on edit switch) + `type="number"` with proper mobile keyboard. Empty value falls back to current time from state instead of "12". Also removed `onBlur` clamping logic (caused the "resets to 12" bug). TS / lint clean.
- 2026-06-17 — feat(infra): Hermes admin tools for dashboard self-management. Added 5 new tools to `src/lib/hermes-tools.ts`: `check_for_update`, `trigger_update`, `get_container_status`, `restart_container`, `check_pocketbase`. Each tool calls the corresponding `/api/admin/*` endpoint via internal HTTP (using `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`). Created `src/app/api/admin/containers/route.ts` (GET — lists Docker containers consuela-dashboard, pocketbase, hermes-agent-2) and `src/app/api/admin/restart/route.ts` (POST — restarts a named container from an allow-list). Updated `src/app/api/hermes/chat/route.ts` system prompts to inform Consuela of her new admin tools and the confirmation-before-action rule. Added `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL` to `.env.docker` and `docker-compose.yml`. AGENTS.md §5 added with full admin capabilities documentation (what Consuela can/can't do). TS / lint clean.
- 2026-06-18 — feat(db): Full PocketBase migration (all 8 phases). PB is now the primary data source; localStorage is cache/fallback. New collections: `schedules`, `auth_sessions`, `tasks`, `week_data`, `week_archive`, `rewards`, `penalties`, `family_goals`, `hall_of_fame`, `recipes` (all seeded in `pb-seed.ts`). CRUD methods added to `pb-db.ts` for each collection. `db/index.ts` updated with member cache refresh (Phase 2), `CacheRefresher.tsx` global component for 60s/visibility-change PB polling (Phases 3+8). `useGrocery.ts`/`usePantry.ts` flipped to PB-first init (Phase 4). `useMeals.ts`/`useRecipes.ts` wired to PB writes (Phase 5). `task-utils.ts` gains 9 PB sync functions; tasks page syncs structured data every 5s (Phase 6). `useAuth.tsx` persists sessions to PB with boot restore (Phase 7). `selectRecipes`/`upsertRecipe`/`deleteRecipe`/`upsertTask`/`upsertWeekData`/`upsertReward`/`upsertPenalty`/`upsertFamilyGoal`/`insertHallOfFameEntry` pass-through methods added to `db/index.ts`. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `eslint` clean (0 errors), `npm run build` clean.
- 2026-06-18 — feat: Push-local-to-PB bulk upload utility. `src/lib/push-local-to-pb.ts` reads 13 localStorage keys and pushes all existing data to PocketBase via existing upsert/insert methods (grocery, pantry, meals, recipes, events, schedules, emergency contacts, family goal, tasks/leaderboard). Settings → Data & sync gains a "☁️ Push local data to cloud" button with toast feedback showing per-collection push counts. `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run lint` clean.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(security): Emergency endpoint now requires PIN authentication. `src/app/api/emergency/route.ts` — POST requests must include `pin` in the JSON body or `x-emergency-pin` header. PIN is verified against any family member's stored PIN. `src/components/ui/EmergencyButton.tsx` updated to send the logged-in user's PIN from auth context. `EMERGENCY_PIN_BYPASS` env var available for testing. Prevents unauthorized SMS/email blasts to emergency contacts.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(security): PIN no longer persisted to localStorage. `src/hooks/useAuth.tsx` — `login()` and `handleMembersUpdated` now write only `id`, `name`, `role`, `emoji`, `color`, `avatarSize`, `glow` to `consuela-auth-user`. The 4-digit PIN is kept in memory only (as `AuthUser.pin`) for the active session. This prevents PIN exposure via browser devtools or localStorage inspection.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(security): Removed real PII from fallback data. `src/db/pb-db.ts` — `emergencyFallback` phone numbers and emails replaced with placeholders (`+15551234567`, `parent1@example.com`). Fallback data is only used when PocketBase is unreachable.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(db): Meal/recipe PB writes now stringify array fields. `src/db/pb-db.ts` — `insertMeal`, `updateMeal`, and `upsertRecipe` now `JSON.stringify()` `ingredients` and `tags` arrays before writing to PocketBase. Previously, PB silently stored `[object Object]` or dropped the field, causing `selectMeals` JSON.parse to fail on reload.
- 2026-07-16 — fix(db): `deleteExpiredAuthSessions` now uses PB server-side filter instead of fetching all records. `src/db/pb-db.ts` — Uses `filter='lastActiveAt < "${cutoff}"'` to let PocketBase handle the filtering, reducing data transfer from O(n) to O(expired).
- 2026-07-16 — fix(tasks): Duplicate task completion guard. `src/app/tasks/page.tsx` — `submitPin` now checks `task.completedInWeek === weekKey()` before allowing completion, preventing the same task from being completed twice in one week even if two devices sync simultaneously.
- 2026-07-17 — feat(ui): Apple-glass material consolidation (Stream A). `src/app/globals.css` — consolidated glass variants into 3 Apple-style materials: `.material-thin` (12px blur, saturate 1.2), `.material-regular` (20px blur, saturate 1.4), `.material-thick` (26px blur, saturate 1.6). Each carries a specular top-light via `::before` (radial white gradient, `mix-blend-mode: screen`) — the Apple "liquid glass" signature. Aliases `.glass` → material-regular, `.glass-subtle` → material-thin, `.glass-strong` → material-thick now share the same selector groups (fixed Tailwind v4 `@apply` incompatibility). Removed neumorphic `.neu-raised`, `.neu-pressed`, `.neu-flat` classes and `--neu-*` variables; `.soft-press` now uses inset shadow instead of `var(--neu-pressed)`. Light-mode + `prefers-color-scheme` overrides added for all 3 materials (fixes old near-white dark-mode bug via alias to material-thick). `[data-contrast="boost"]` rule now thickens borders + brightens border color instead of dimming all UI to 0.7 opacity. `src/components/ui/Surface.tsx` — removed `neu`/`neu-pressed`/`flat` variants, added `material-regular`/`material-thick`; `flat` maps to `material-regular` with surface bg. `src/components/ui/Modal.tsx` — modal now uses `material-thick` (translucent `surface-0/80` + `backdrop-blur-2xl`) with spring entrance via `modalEnter` keyframe (cubic-bezier overshoot). `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx` — neutral tone uses `surface-0/80`; slide-down spring entrance via `toastSlide` keyframe. `src/components/ui/SoftButton.tsx` / `IconButton.tsx` / `Chip.tsx` / `ListRow.tsx` / `Surface.tsx` — unified press scale to `active:scale-[0.97]`. Added `modalEnter` + `toastSlide` keyframes to `globals.css`. `src/app/design-system/page.tsx` — demo cards updated from `neu`/`neu-pressed` to `material-regular`/`material-thick`. TS / lint / build clean.

Deleted `src/services/types.ts`. `src/db/index.ts` — `selectMeals`/`selectPantry`/`selectGrocery` made async passthroughs (was sync cache-read); `pantryStore`/`mealsStore`/`groceryStore` for sync useMemo access. `src/services/mealSync.ts` — `updateGroceryItem` now always writes to PB (was in-memory-only); `calculateDeficit` uses real pantry quantities; ingredient deficits accumulated+deduped across all planned meals before syncing. `src/lib/pb-seed.ts` — added `protein`/`carbs`/`fat` to `meal_plan_entries`, `recipeId`/`recipeSnapshotAt`/`weekOf`/`date`, `meal_week_archive` collection, `quantity`/`unit` to `pantry_items`, `quantityValue`/`unit`/`pinned` to `grocery_list_items`. `scripts/pb-seed.mjs` — rewritten as tsx wrapper importing from `pb-seed.ts` (Option A); `npm run pb:seed` updated. `src/lib/pb-auth.ts` — removed hardcoded password, requires `PB_ADMIN_EMAIL`/`PB_ADMIN_PASS` env vars. `src/hooks/useMeals.ts` — init now async (`.then()`), added `activeWeek`, `goToWeek`, `archiveCurrentWeek`, `isCurrentWeek`. `src/hooks/useGrocery.ts` + `usePantry.ts` — init now async. `src/lib/meals-week-utils.ts` — week math (mirrors `task-utils.ts`). `src/app/meals/archive/page.tsx` — archive viewer with restore. `src/components/meals/MealsTab.tsx` — week nav header, `resolveMealEntry` for recipe-as-reference. `src/components/meals/RecipesTab.tsx` — day-picker passes `day`, file input IDs fixed, orphaned bottom input removed. `src/hooks/useRecipes.ts` — `saveCatalogRecipe` propagates edits to linked meals. `src/app/meals/page.tsx` — `weekOf` set on all meal creation; copy/duplicate scoped to current week. `src/lib/recipe-pantry-match.ts` — pantry-aware recipe readiness + catalog finder. `src/app/api/recipes/ingest/route.ts` — replaced Playwright with fetch+Readability+linkedom, IP blocking, Schema.org LD+JSON extraction. Deleted `/api/meals`, `/api/sync`. `src/app/api/recipes/ingest` — no Playwright. Installed `@mozilla/readability` + `linkedom`. `MEAL_SYSTEM_ARCHITECTURE.md` replaced with current-state doc. TS / lint (0 errors, 12 pre-existing warnings) / build clean.

Behavior: `overwrite=true` (future weeks) clears the target week first; `overwrite=false` (current week) fills empty slots only. New state `weeklyPlanLoading`/`weeklyPlanError` returned. `src/components/meals/MealsTab.tsx` — "✨ Generate" button in the week nav header (between Archive and next-week arrow); disabled + "Generating..." while running; inline error pill on failure; success toast via new `showToast` prop (passed from `page.tsx`). `src/app/meals/page.tsx` — destructured + passed `generateWeeklyPlan`/`weeklyPlanLoading`/`weeklyPlanError`/`showToast` to MealsTab. Two-week planning workflow: plan this week's real meals, tap › to next week, Generate for a full AI-planned blank week. TS / lint / build clean.

# This is NOT the Next.js you know

This version has breaking changes — APIs, conventions, and file structure may all differ from your training data.
